*Ksenia Merzlyakova,Sebastian Padó,Franziska Weeber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了回答格式（封闭式、李克特量表、开放式）对大语言模型（LLM）性别偏见测量的影响，发现格式显著改变测量结果，甚至导致排名反转，呼吁将回答格式视为评估的核心组件并采用多格式设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调查科学早已知晓回答格式对人类答案有重大影响，LLM 对提示词也敏感，但此前未系统研究回答格式变化如何影响 LLM 性别偏见的测量及其与人类回答分布的对齐。

Method: 在 BBQ 基准和 OpinionQA 调查数据上，对三个指令微调模型分别使用封闭式、李克特量表、开放式三种回答格式进行评测，保持其余条件相同，对比偏见测量结果与分布对齐情况。

Result: 回答格式显著改变测量结果，包括偏见排名的反转。差异源于不同格式诱发的不同响应行为：封闭式导致强制选择，李克特量表产生量表分布，开放式则出现自由文本生成中的拒答现象。

Conclusion: 回答格式应被视为 LLM 评估的实质性组件，建议采用多格式评测设计以获得更稳健的模型评估。

Abstract: Gender bias or other social biases in large language models (LLMs) are frequently evaluated with question answering or survey benchmarks where the LLM needs to give a response in a predefined answer format. It is well known in survey science that the answer format has a substantial impact on answers, just as LLMs are sensitive to the prompt wording. However, to our knowledge it has not been studied yet how changes in answer format impact the measurement of gender bias in LLMs and their alignment with human response distributions. We evaluate three instruction-tuned models on the BBQ benchmark and OpinionQA survey data across closed-ended, Likert-scaled and open-ended formats, comparing bias measurement and distributional alignment under otherwise identical conditions. We find that answer format does substantially alter measured outcomes, including reversals in order rankings. These differences arise because each format elicits distinct response behaviours, such as forced-choice selection, scale-based distributions and refusal in free-text generation. Our findings highlight the importance of treating answer format as a substantive component of LLM evaluation and motivate multi-format designs for more robust model assessment.

</details>


### [24] [ArborMem: Navigating Interaction States with Memory Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17534)
*Zongwei Lv,Yuemeng Xu,Yilun Yao,Siyi Ding,Xinyu Tan,Yaoming Li,Guangxiang Zhao,Weihong Lin,Lin Sun,Xiangzheng Zhang,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ArborMem，一种将长期对话建模为可导航的交互状态森林的在线记忆框架，并引入 BranchMemEval 基准评估分支结构记忆能力。实验表明 ArborMem 在多个长时记忆基准上显著优于现有最强基线，且在受限读取预算下优势更大，查询延迟低于半秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时记忆方法主要关注从对话历史中检索相关信息，但忽略了首先确定当前轮次恢复的是哪个先前交互状态的问题。当对话交织多个任务、人物和计划，且可能被中断后恢复时，这种限制尤为突出。需要一种能够区分语义相关但结构不同的交互轨迹、并支持分支恢复的记忆机制。

Method: ArborMem 将长期对话表示为交互状态的可导航森林。每个分支保持局部连贯的轨迹，森林维护多个可后续恢复的轨迹。对于每个新输入，ArborMem 定位相关状态，恢复其分支局部上下文，并通过跨分支检索可复用证据进行增强，从而在不混淆语义相关但结构不同的轨迹的前提下保持交互连续性。同时引入 BranchMemEval，一个专门针对交织和可恢复交互轨迹的受控诊断基准。

Result: 在 LongMemEval、LoCoMo、BEAM 100K 和 BranchMemEval 四个基准上，ArborMem 比最强基线高出 3.36 到 10.31 个百分点（三个现有基准）和 5.0 个百分点。在受限读取预算下优势扩大，完整记忆查询延迟保持在半秒以内。

Conclusion: ArborMem 通过分支森林结构显式建模对话的多轨迹特性，有效解决了长期对话中交织任务的状态恢复问题。其设计在保持低延迟的同时显著提升记忆检索与推理准确性，为持久化对话助手的长时记忆提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly serve as persistent conversational assistants, requiring memory that preserves relevant experience and maintains continuity across interactions. Existing methods improve access to conversational history through long-context processing, selective retrieval, and structured memory organization. However, most systems treat memory access as retrieving relevant past information without first determining which prior interaction state the current turn resumes. This limitation becomes particularly important when conversations interleave multiple tasks, people, and plans that may be interrupted and later revisited. We introduce ArborMem, an online memory framework that represents a long-running conversation as a navigable forest of interaction states. Each branch preserves a locally coherent trajectory, while the forest maintains multiple trajectories that may later be resumed. For each new input, ArborMem localizes the relevant state, restores its branch-local context, and augments it with reusable evidence retrieved across branches, preserving interaction continuity without conflating semantically related but structurally distinct trajectories. Existing long-term memory benchmarks cover diverse memory and reasoning capabilities but do not explicitly isolate branch-structured challenges. We therefore introduce BranchMemEval, a controlled diagnostic benchmark for interleaved and resumable interaction trajectories. Experiments on LongMemEval, LoCoMo, BEAM 100K, and BranchMemEval show that ArborMem outperforms the strongest baselines by 3.36 to 10.31 percentage points on the three established benchmarks and by 5.0 points on BranchMemEval. Its advantage grows under constrained read budgets, while complete memory queries remain below half a second.

</details>


### [25] [CoAL-RAG: A Complexity-Aware Legal Retrieval-Augmented Generation Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17536)
*Jin Su,Zhuofeng Zhao,Huanhuan Wang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoAL-RAG 是一种复杂度感知的法律检索增强生成方法，通过多维评估机制自适应选择检索策略，在中英文法律基准上显著优于基线，实现生成质量、逻辑推理与系统效率的最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律咨询问题具有多层次复杂性，单一检索策略会导致简单问题过度推理、复杂问题可解释性差，难以在高风险场景下同时满足答案质量和效率要求。

Method: 提出 CoAL-RAG 方法，构建基于「问题本质」和「检索一致性」的多维评估机制，实现检索策略自适应路由。首先根据问题逻辑结构量化推理需求，再利用语义检索与关键词检索的差异间接反映问题复杂度，从而选择最合适的检索策略并动态过滤上下文信息。

Result: 在中文法律基准（SocialLawQA、LawBench）上显著优于基线模型，BLEU 提升 42.5%，ROUGE-L 达到知识图谱方法的 3.6 倍；在英文基准（LexGLUE、CaseHold）上保持高竞争力准确率，展现强跨司法管辖区泛化能力，在不同法系间实现生成质量、深度逻辑推理与系统效率的最优平衡。

Conclusion: CoAL-RAG 通过复杂度感知的自适应检索策略选择，有效解决了法律问答中的复杂性问题，同时提升了回答质量与系统效率，并具备跨法域泛化能力。}

Abstract: Legal consultation questions exhibit multi-level complexity. A single retrieval strategy often leads to over-reasoning for simple questions and poor interpretability for complex ones, making it difficult to meet the requirements for both answer quality and efficiency in high-risk scenarios. To address this issue, this paper proposes CoAL-RAG, a complexity-aware legal retrieval-augmented generation method, which constructs a multi-dimensional evaluation mechanism based on ``question essence'' and ``retrieval consistency'' to enable adaptive routing of retrieval strategies. First, the reasoning demand is quantified according to the logical structure of the question. Then, the discrepancy between semantic retrieval and keyword retrieval is utilized to indirectly reflect problem complexity, thereby selecting the most appropriate retrieval strategy and dynamically filtering contextual information. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms baseline models not only on Chinese legal benchmarks (SocialLawQA, LawBench) but also demonstrates strong cross-jurisdictional generalization on English datasets (LexGLUE, CaseHold). Specifically, on Chinese datasets, the BLEU score improves by 42.5\% and ROUGE-L reaches 3.6 times that of knowledge graph-based methods. On English benchmarks, CoAL-RAG maintains highly competitive accuracy, achieving an optimal balance between generation quality, deep logical reasoning, and system efficiency across different legal systems.

</details>


### [26] [Auditing Exposure to Harmful Content on TikTok using Multimodal Language Models: A Cross-National, Age-Stratified Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17583)
*Hamidreza Saffari,Francesco Pierri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过多模态大语言模型（MLLM）审计 TikTok 在法国、意大利、瑞典三国对四个年龄人设（13、16、19、40 岁）的有害内容推荐情况，发现关键词搜索显著增加有害内容曝光（35-56%），被动滚动下意大利危害率最高（19 岁达 48.6%），平台安全过滤仅拒绝 1.1% 且漏判最露骨危害。MLLM 标注成本低（约 $50 覆盖 10% 样本），kappa=0.42，为跨国青少年安全审计提供可扩展方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线视频平台易向未成年人推送有害内容，但独立审计受限于视频标注成本高昂、多语言审核判断不一致，缺乏可扩展、跨国的量化评估手段。

Method: 1. 构建袜子账号模拟四年龄人设，在法、意、瑞三国采集 36,971 条视频（被动 For-You 推荐流 + 主动 搜索危害关键词→再滚动）。2. 建立 300 条母语者标注基准集，对比四款 MLLM，选 Gemini 2.5 Flash（8 帧+文本，kappa=0.42，成本仅为原生视频上传一半）对 10% 样本推理，总 API 费用约 $50。

Result: 关键词搜索使有害内容比例升至 35%-56%，为滚动基线 1.5-7.5 倍（12 组中 10 组显著）；冲击为暂时性，并抹平法、瑞两国的年龄差异。被动滚动下意大利全年龄危害率最高，19 岁达 48.6%。平台自带安全过滤拒绝率仅 1.1%，且对最露骨危害漏判严重。

Conclusion: 基于 MLLM 的审计框架低成本、可跨语言扩展，适合大规模青少年安全跨国审计；现有平台级过滤机制对显性有害内容覆盖不足，需更透明的第三方持续监测。

Abstract: Online video platforms can expose young users to harmful content, but independent audits remain difficult because video annotation is costly and moderation judgments vary across languages. We audit TikTok in France, Italy, and Sweden with sockpuppet accounts representing four age personas (13, 16, 19, 40), collecting 36,971 videos from passive For-You-page scrolling and active sessions that scroll, search for harm keywords, and scroll again. To scale annotation, we validate four multimodal LLMs against native-speaker labels on a 300-video reference set. Gemini 2.5 Flash with eight sampled frames plus text performs best (aggregate kappa = 0.42), at half the per-call cost of native-video upload, and we apply it to a 10% sample for approximately \$50 in total API spend across both modalities. Keyword search returns 35-56% harmful content, a 1.5-7.5x increase over the scrolling baseline in ten of twelve country-age combinations; the spike is temporary and flattens the age differences observed in France and Sweden. Under passive scrolling, Italy has the highest harm rate at every age, with Italian age-19 reaching 48.6%. Overall, MLLM-based auditing offers a scalable approach for cross-national youth-safety audits, while provider safety filters (1.1% refusal rate) under-count the most explicit harms.

</details>


### [27] [Write, Execute, Refine: From Skill Followers to Skill Optimizers via Reinforcement Learning from Execution Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17587)
*Kang Peng,Zhiwei Zhang,Yichen Zhang,Zezhong Wang,Yiming Du,Geng Tu,Baojun Wang,Bin Liang,Ruifeng Xu,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 WER (Write, Execute, and Refine) 多阶段框架，在冻结执行器外部训练技能优化器，将中间技能的执行经验组织为训练状态，使优化器从自身输出的后果中学习。在 BFCL v4 multi-turn 和 tau2-bench 上，WER 显著优于无技能基线和无优化器训练的骨干模型，训练后的 4B 优化器达到 76.63% Pass@1，平均超越所有评测的现成通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家编写的自然语言技能能提升工具使用代理，但代理自行编写的技能反而比无技能基线差 8-11 分，揭示了遵循程序指导和从执行证据中改进指导是两种不同能力。推理时循环虽能修复技能，但不能改进编写下一个技能的模型，因此需要将中间技能的执行经验组织为优化器的训练状态。

Method: WER 框架包含三阶段：优化器提出技能，冻结代理重复执行每个技能，程序化验证器对结果评分。评分提供相对信用并选择混合结果记录，将匹配的成功和失败轨迹配对形成下一阶段的细化状态，使优化器从早期输出的后果中学习。

Result: 在 BFCL v4 multi-turn 和 tau2-bench 上，WER 相较无技能基线平均 Pass@1 分别提升 7.80 和 3.85 分。在相同细化工作流下，优于无优化器训练的相同骨干模型 9.35 和 10.29 分。4B 优化器在 BFCL v4 达到 76.63% Pass@1，平均超越所有评测的通用模型作为技能优化器的表现。

Conclusion: WER 有效解决了代理自行编写技能性能差的问题，通过将执行经验转化为优化器训练状态实现自我改进，显著提升多轮工具使用任务性能，且小参数模型即可超越大型通用模型。

Abstract: Expert-written natural language skills can improve tool-using agents, yet agent-authored skills perform 8-11 points worse than using no skill. This gap suggests that following procedural guidance and improving it from execution evidence are distinct capabilities. Inference time loops can repair skills but do not improve the model that writes the next one. We study how to organize execution experience from intermediate skills into training states for an optimizer. We introduce WER (Write, Execute, and Refine), a multi-phase framework that trains a Skill Optimizer outside a frozen executor. The optimizer proposes skills, a frozen agent executes each repeatedly, and a programmatic verifier scores the outcomes. The scores provide relative credit and select mixed-outcome records. Matched successful and failed trajectories from these records form the next phase's refinement states, so the optimizer learns from the consequences of its earlier outputs. On BFCL v4 multi-turn and tau2-bench, WER improves average Pass@1 over the no-skill baseline by 7.80 and 3.85 points, respectively. Under an identical refinement workflow, it outperforms the same backbone without optimizer training by 9.35 and 10.29 points. The trained 4B optimizer reaches 76.63 percent on BFCL v4, outperforming all evaluated off-the-shelf general-purpose models used as skill optimizers on average.

</details>


### [28] [Multi-turn Conversational AI from Text to Multimodal Interaction: Data, Models, Evaluation, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17605)
*Syeda Faiza Ahmed,Zien Sheikh Ali,Hunzalah Hassan Bhatti,Firoj Alam,Shammur Absar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了多轮对话 AI 的最新进展，涵盖纯文本、音频大语言模型、多模态/全模态系统及工具增强智能体，指出当前系统在多模态感知与行动能力增强的同时，仍难以在长会话中保持连贯交互、持久记忆、跨轮定基、全双工交互、鲁棒评估及文化对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实对话中用户会不断澄清目标、修改请求、打断回复、切换话题并引入新证据，要求系统跨轮保持上下文；多轮对话因此成为涉及记忆维护、多模态定基、工具调用及跨文化适应的独特挑战，亟需系统性梳理现有研究现状与不足。

Method: 采用文献综述法，按数据集与基准、建模范式、训练策略、评估体系及跨领域挑战五个维度组织文献，分别对纯文本对话、AudioLLMs 与语音原生系统、多模态/全模态系统、工具增强智能体四大类系统进行分类分析。

Result: 多模态支持的进展快于跨会话连贯交互能力；现有系统虽在感知、发言、跨模态行动上增强，但在持久记忆、跨轮定基、全双工交互、鲁棒评估及文化对齐等核心问题上仍显不足。

Conclusion: 提出面向未来的研究议程，呼吁构建能在多轮、多模态、多文化场景下实现记忆、修正、定基、听说、行动与自适应的新一代对话 AI 系统。

Abstract: Conversational AI is moving beyond isolated text prompts toward sustained, multimodal interaction. In real conversations, users clarify goals, revise requests, interrupt responses, switch topics, and introduce new evidence while expecting systems to preserve context across turns. This makes multi-turn dialogue a distinct challenge requiring systems to maintain and update memory, ground responses across modalities, tools, and external knowledge, and adapt across languages and cultures. This study reviews multi-turn conversational AI across text-only dialogue, AudioLLMs and speech-native systems, multimodal and omni-modal systems, and tool-augmented agents. We organize the literature around datasets and benchmarks, modeling paradigms, training strategies, evaluation setups, and cross-cutting challenges. Our analysis shows that support for multiple modalities has advanced faster than the ability to sustain coherent interaction across a session. Despite stronger capabilities to perceive, speak, and act across modalities, current systems still struggle with persistent memory, cross-turn grounding, full-duplex interaction, robust evaluation, and cultural alignment. We conclude with a research agenda for systems that can remember, revise, ground, speak, listen, act, and adapt across turns, modalities, and cultures. (https://github.com/faiza-sfa/multiturn-conversational-ai-survey)

</details>


### [29] [Thinking in a Low-Resource Language: What SFT Builds, What RL Fixes, What Accuracy Cannot See](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17744)
*Ayoub Kirouane,Christos Petrocheilos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在低资源语言（希腊语）下对三个前沿混合专家模型进行微调的效果。发现准确率基准测试在该规模下噪声极大（随机种子变化导致7.7分波动），但通过监督微调（SFT）可使模型以目标语言进行推理（98%覆盖率），且不损害通用能力。进一步引入可验证奖励的强化学习（RL）修复了SFT遗留的格式缺失、推理泄露等问题，且希腊语推理习惯在仅优化准确率的梯度下保持不变。研究提出六个行为维度度量框架并发布五个检查点，方法可迁移至任意低资源语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在低资源语言下虽能给出正确答案，但推理过程使用用户不懂的语言（如英语），导致不可审计、不可纠正。现有准确率基准在小规模下噪声过大，无法捕捉推理语言、格式遵循、泄露控制等关键行为变化。需建立可靠的行为度量维度与控制实验，验证微调与强化学习对低资源语言推理能力的真实提升。

Method: 1. 选取三个前沿MoE模型（阿里巴巴、OpenAI、NVIDIA，3.6-4.0B激活参数），在希腊语数据上进行监督微调（SFT）。2. 提出六个行为维度（推理语言一致性、格式遵循、推理泄露、语法性、令牌效率、通用能力保持），每个维度设置长度相关性门控以剔除虚假相关。3. 引入预注册的可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR），对比平坦随机奖励控制组。4. 使用1000条推理轨迹人工评估，发布五个检查点及完整实验工具链。

Result: 1. 准确率基准无显著变化（随机种子波动7.7分 > 所有数据/配方效应）。2. 基础模型0/1000用希腊语推理；SFT后所有检查点~98%用提问语言推理，一模型家族令牌数减少3倍，语法性全面提升，通用能力保持在基础模型几分内。3. 六个行为维度中发现六次仪器失效，均被控制实验捕获。4. SFT遗留缺陷：24%格式缺失、3.5%推理泄露、<50%遵循“用英语思考”指令。5. RLVR将格式缺失降至2.5%，泄露归零，指令遵循+9.1个百分点；仅优化准确率的梯度下希腊语推理习惯不变。

Conclusion: 在低资源语言微调中，准确率基准失效，需依赖行为维度度量。SFT可高效赋予目标语言推理能力且不遗忘通用知识；RLVR能修复格式、泄露等结构性缺陷。提出的六维度框架、控制实验及预注册流程可迁移至任意低资源语言，希腊语仅为验证案例。

Abstract: Take three frontier mixture-of-experts models (Alibaba, OpenAI, NVIDIA; 3.6-4.0B active parameters each) and fine-tune them to reason in a low-resource language. On accuracy benchmarks almost nothing happens, and the benchmark itself is noise at this scale: changing only the random seed moves the score by 7.7 points, more than every data and recipe effect we measured. That null is our first result. The real changes live where accuracy cannot see. Base models never think in Greek: 0 of 1,000 reasoning traces, even when the question is Greek, so the model answers correctly while reasoning in a form its user cannot read, audit, or correct. After supervised fine-tuning (SFT), every released checkpoint reasons in the language of the question on ~98% of items, one family at 3x fewer tokens, with judged grammaticality improving on all four models and general ability within a few points of each base: nothing was forgotten, and fluency was gained. We propose six behavioural dimensions that make such changes measurable, each gated to reject any metric that correlates with output length, and we report how our own instruments lied: six failures, each caught by a control. What SFT cannot do is fix its own defects: a quarter of answers skip the requested format, answers leak into the reasoning channel, and an explicit "think in English" is obeyed under half the time. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, pre-registered before training, fixes the first two outright (fallback 24% to 2.5%, leak 3.5% to 0.0%, both against a flat random-reward control) and moves the third (+9.1pp), while the Greek reasoning habit survives an accuracy-only gradient untouched. We release five checkpoints. The instruments, the controls and the pre-registration travel to any low-resource language; Greek is the case that let us measure them.

</details>


### [30] [Preference Is Not Intervention: The Structure and Stability Boundaries of Reader-Specific Evidence Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17781)
*Shi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究检索增强生成(RAG)中阅读器模型对证据效用的异质性，发现序数偏好几何结构稳定但符号方向几何受任务限制，稳定的序数相似性不能预测跨阅读器干预迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究模型特定差异是否形成可复用结构而非输入局部交互，即阅读器模型对证据效用的判断是否存在稳定、可迁移的模式。

Method: 在控制查询、证据、任务、评分和干预固定的条件下，测试九个阅读器模型；分离证据活性、序数偏好、条件符号方向三个可测量对象；在四种独立设置(留一干预、PRISM偏好、RAMDocs、RAGuard)中评估稳定性；分析稀疏性、解码噪声、度量伪影对序数-符号差距的影响；测试序数相似性对跨阅读器干预迁移的预测力。

Result: 九个阅读器在33%联合影响单元上对效用符号存在分歧；阅读器×查询交互解释29.8%效用方差；自选证据提升F1 +0.031；序数阅读器几何在四设置中稳定(分半ρ=0.60-0.83)；符号几何受任务限制：开放域问答弱(0.14,0.35)，二元事实核查强(0.75)；稀疏性、解码噪声、度量伪影不能解释主要序数-符号差距；稳定序数相似性不能预测跨阅读器干预迁移(甲骨距离ρ=-0.27；遗憾可靠性-0.28)。

Conclusion: 阅读器特定效用存在，但偏好不等于干预：稳定的排序相似性不授权帮助/伤害决策的迁移。

Abstract: ML systems increasingly condition decisions on downstream model identity, but this is useful only if model-specific differences form reusable structure rather than input-local interactions. We test this in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), where evidence utility can be measured under controlled interventions. Holding query, evidence, task, scoring, and intervention fixed, nine readers disagree on effect sign in 33\% of jointly affected cells; reader$\times$query interaction explains 29.8\% of utility variance versus an 8.4\% permutation null; and self-selected evidence improves F1 by $+0.031$ ($t=3.39$). We then ask the sharper question: \emph{which components of this heterogeneity are stable reader properties across queries?} Separating three measurable objects---evidence \emph{activity}, \emph{ordinal preference}, and \emph{conditional signed direction}---we find ordinal reader geometry stable across four independent settings (split-half $ρ=0.60$--$0.83$): leave-one-out interventions, PRISM preferences, RAMDocs, and RAGuard. Signed geometry is task-bounded: weak in open-ended QA (0.14, 0.35), especially for misleading and irrelevant evidence, but strong in binary fact-checking (0.75) with no significant ordinal gap, though still below its sparsity-matched ceiling. Sparsity, decoding noise, and metric artifacts do not explain the main ordinal--signed gap. Finally, stable ordinal similarity fails to predict cross-reader intervention transfer (oracle-distance $ρ=-0.27$; regret reliability $-0.28$). Reader-specific utility exists, but preference is not intervention: stable ranking similarity does not license transfer of help/harm decisions.

</details>


### [31] [TraceSQL: Traceable Answerability Estimation for Reference-Free Text-to-SQL Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17795)
*Neelesh Kumar Shukla,Debasmita Panda,Srutanik Bhaduri,Aditya Banerjee,Viji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceSQL，一种基于67个显式诊断特征的轻量级可追踪SQL验证模型，解决了推理阶段缺乏真实SQL监督的验证问题，在BIRD数据集上超越ORM基线，且提供特征级证据支持决策溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL系统在推理时缺乏真实SQL或执行结果监督，难以判断生成SQL是否正确回答用户问题。现有方法（LLM裁判、专用代理、结果奖励模型ORM）虽可估计正确性，但决策过程不透明，缺乏可追踪的诊断信号。

Method: TraceSQL构建67个显式诊断特征，涵盖问题歧义性、问题需求、问题-模式-SQL一致性、SQL结构和意图对齐五大维度。这些特征作为轻量级学习模型的输入，保持特征级可解释性，支持决策回溯到诊断证据。

Result: 在BIRD开发数据库上，TraceSQL达到66.47% F1和64.48% ROC-AUC，优于GradeSQL-7B ORM基线的61.87% F1和58.26% ROC-AUC。特征归因显示模型同时依赖语义落地信号和确定性SQL结构信号。

Conclusion: SQL验证可通过轻量级学习模型实现，同时保留特征级证据，使预测可被检查和诊断，为实际部署提供了可追踪的验证方案。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL systems are commonly evaluated using ground-truth SQL queries or reference execution results, but such supervision is unavailable at inference time in real-world deployments. This creates a critical verification problem: given only a user question, database context, and generated SQL, can a system estimate whether the generated query is likely to correctly answer the question? Recent approaches use LLMs as judge or specialized agents to inspect generated SQL, but their decisions can be difficult to trace. Outcome Reward Models (ORMs) address this by learning from execution-labeled candidate SQLs and assigning correctness scores to unseen queries, yet they still provide limited visibility into the signals behind each verification. To address this limitation, we propose TraceSQL, a lightweight and traceable verification model built on explicit diagnostic features. TraceSQL combines 67 features capturing question ambiguity, question requirements, question-schema-SQL consistency, SQL structure, and intent alignment. These signals remain available for examining which factors influence each prediction and for tracing decisions back to diagnostic evidence. On BIRD development databases, TraceSQL achieves 66.47% F1 and 64.48% ROC-AUC, compared with 61.87% F1 and 58.26% ROC-AUC for the GradeSQL-7B ORM baseline on the same generated-SQL evaluation. Feature attribution further shows that the model relies on both semantic grounding and deterministic SQL-structure signals. These results show that SQL verification can be performed with a lightweight learned model while retaining feature-level evidence for inspecting and diagnosing its predictions.

</details>


### [32] [Whether LLMs Can Navigate Beliefs and Facts Depends on How You Phrase It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17809)
*Quang Minh Nguyen,Luis Frentzen Salim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了10个大语言模型在18种认知表达（如'I think'、'I suppose'）下处理用户信念的能力，发现模型在面对错误信息时存在系统性弱点，且表现强烈依赖于所用动词；核心原因是模型默认将任务理解为事实核查而非信念追踪，思维链中的显性事实核查反而降低准确率；单条指令可在一定程度上逆转该失败模式，但抑制注意力机制仅能部分恢复准确率，揭示了事实核查行为与信念追踪之间的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类日常交流中自然地形成并表达信念，这些信念常与事实和知识交织。随着大语言模型广泛部署于用户交互场景，模型需同时处理事实与用户信念。既往研究表明即使强模型也难以确认基于错误信息的用户信念，但缺乏对不同认知动词的系统性评估及机制解释。

Method: 选取10个大语言模型，构建包含18种认知表达（如'I vaguely remember'、'I seriously doubt'）的评测集，对比模型在事实性信息与虚假信息下的准确率差距；分析思维链中是否显性执行事实核查对性能的影响；通过指令干预尝试逆转失败模式；利用注意力机制分析模型对虚假信念的关注模式，并在解码时抑制相关注意力以观察恢复效果。

Result: 准确率差距随动词显著变化：'I vaguely remember' 为 +50%（倾向接受虚假信息），'I seriously doubt' 为 -14%（倾向拒绝虚假信息）；模型默认将任务理解为事实核查，覆盖用户陈述的信念；显性事实核查的思维链在虚假信息上准确率更低；单条指令可跨动词族逆转失败；模型对无法确认的虚假信念分配更多注意力，解码时抑制该注意力仅在部分模型中部分恢复准确率。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了既往结果，揭示了事实核查这一通常期望的行为如何干扰大语言模型的信念追踪能力；指出当前干预手段（如注意力抑制）效果有限，呼吁未来开发更有效的干预方法以平衡事实核查与信念追踪。

Abstract: Humans naturally form and express beliefs in daily communication, e.g., "I think the answer is 3" or "I suppose that's right." Such beliefs inevitably intertwine with fact and knowledge, making the ability to handle them in tandem desirable for large language models (LLMs), as they are increasingly deployed in user-facing settings. Prior work showed that even capable LLMs exhibit a systemic weakness in acknowledging user beliefs grounded in incorrect information. We extend this evaluation to 10 LLMs across 18 epistemic expressions and find that the size and direction of the weakness depend on the verb used to express the belief, with the accuracy gap between factual and false information ranging from +50% on "I vaguely remember" to -14% on "I seriously doubt". We further show that the phenomenon stems from task confusion: models default to fact-checking the underlying claim, overriding the user's stated belief; chains of thought that explicitly fact-check show lower accuracy on false information than those that do not; and a single instruction can reverse the failure across verb families. Mechanistically, models attend more to false beliefs they fail to confirm, but suppressing this attention at decoding time recovers accuracy only partially and only in some models, calling for future work on intervention methods. Our findings clarify prior results and show how fact-checking, a generally desirable behavior, can interfere with belief tracking in LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/ngqm/belief-fact-phrasing.

</details>


### [33] [Interpretable Humans, Alien LLMs: Expert Analysis of Latent Structures in Assessment Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17810)
*Alona Strugatski,Licol Zeinfeld,Jason Cooper,Shelley Rap,Gil Schwarts,Giora Alexandron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过探索性因子分析和专家盲评，发现大语言模型（LLM）在定量推理和化学测评中的潜在因子缺乏人类可解释的认知意义，表明LLM的运作机制与人类推理存在本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估依赖人类设计的测评，隐含假设AI与人类共享相似的认知构念。本文旨在挑战这一假设，探究LLM表现背后的潜在因子是否具有与人类学习者认知构念相同的实质性、可解释意义。

Method: 收集人类与六个LLM在定量推理和化学测评上的回答数据，分别对两组数据进行探索性因子分析（EFA），随后邀请学科专家（SME）对生成的因子图进行盲评，尝试赋予其教学含义。

Result: 专家成功解读了大多数人类来源的因子；但在定量推理任务中无法解释任何LLM来源因子，在化学任务中仅能解释约一半LLM因子。

Conclusion: 结合数据驱动的EFA与盲评专家解读，该框架揭示LLM常依赖统计上不透明的机制，这些机制与人类推理的认知构念有本质区别。

Abstract: The evaluation of large language models (LLMs) relies heavily on human-designed assessments, implicitly assuming that AI and humans employ similar underlying cognitive constructs. Challenging this assumption, we investigate whether the latent factors governing LLM performance carry the same substantive, human-interpretable meaning as the cognitive constructs governing human learners. Using responses from humans and six LLMs across quantitative reasoning and chemistry assessments, we conducted Exploratory Factor Analysis (EFA) separately for both groups. Subject-Matter Experts (SMEs) then blindly evaluated the resulting factor graphs to ascribe pedagogical meaning to the emerged constructs. SMEs successfully interpreted most of the human-derived factors. Conversely, they could not ascribe meaning to any LLM-derived factors in quantitative reasoning and interpreted only half of the LLM factors in chemistry. By combining data-driven EFA with blind expert interpretation, this framework shows that LLMs frequently operate on statistically opaque mechanisms distinct from human reasoning.

</details>


### [34] [From Global Benchmarks to Local Evaluations: Benchmarking LLMs for the German Public Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17827)
*Camilla Dalerci,Thilo Michael,Robin Schaefer,Daniel Weinland*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MÖVE 框架，针对德国公共部门评估大语言模型，关注能耗、提供商透明度和德国政党立场知识三个治理维度，发现模型间存在显著权衡，单一模型无法在所有维度表现优异，选型不能仅依赖性能排名，需结合部署语境的治理需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共机构在选择适配自身语境的大语言模型时面临持续挑战，现有基准多为英语和美国中心视角，且仅评估任务性能，忽视了治理相关维度，因此需要一种全面评估框架来满足公共部门的特定需求。

Method: 构建 MÖVE（一种面向德国公共部门的整体评估框架），从能耗、提供商透明度、德国政党立场知识三个罕见考量的治理维度对多个模型进行评估。

Result: 评估结果显示显著的权衡：估算能耗差异超 60 倍且不单由模型规模决定；信息披露在不同提供商间系统性差异；欧洲模型并未表现出更强的德国政党立场知识。

Conclusion: 公共机构的模型选型不应仅依赖性能排名，评估体系必须纳入部署语境的治理要求，以实现负责任且适配的模型部署。

Abstract: Public institutions face a persistent challenge in selecting LLMs suited to their specific context. Existing benchmarks, however, are of limited use as they primarily reflect English-language and US-centric settings, and often only evaluate task performance. In this paper, we present first results of MÖVE, a holistic evaluation framework for the German public sector, examining three rarely considered governance dimensions: energy consumption, provider transparency, and knowledge of German-party positions. Our results reveal significant trade-offs, with no single model excelling across all dimensions: estimated energy consumption varies more than 60-fold and is not explained by model size alone, information disclosure varies systematically across providers, and European models do not exhibit stronger knowledge of German party positions. Model selection for public institutions thus cannot rely on performance rankings alone. Instead, evaluations should also reflect the governance requirements of the deployment context.

</details>


### [35] [Encoded but Not Actionable: Auditing the Decode-Generate-Steer Gap in Frozen LLMs for Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17843)
*Man Liang,Xinzhao Cheng,Faizan Wajid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过几何推理任务（参数化CAD约束）探究大语言模型（LLMs）内部表示的编码情况，发现预训练显著提升了局部几何关系的线性可解码性，但草图级自由度（DOF）状态在随机初始化表示中已高度可解码，且预训练仅带来微小提升。进一步分析显示，可解码信息不一定可被模型表达或控制：生成常失败、激活修补效应消失、均值差异引导不可靠。四种属性（可解码性、生成、激活层面影响、可引导性）在测试设置中可能发散，该审计框架可区分编码失败与表达/控制失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在结构化推理任务上表现强劲，但其内部编码了什么几何结构、以及这些编码是否真正指导模型行为，仍不清楚。作者旨在通过一个受控的几何推理测试床（参数化CAD约束），将局部成对关系与草图级约束状态分离，系统考察预训练对几何结构编码的影响，以及可解码信息与模型实际行为（生成、干预、引导）之间的关系。

Method: 使用六个冻结的仅解码器LLMs，在参数化CAD约束任务上探测隐藏状态。考察四个性质：线性可解码性、强制选择生成、激活层面影响（激活修补/恢复）、行为可引导性（均值差异引导）。引入打乱顺序控制以排除位置线索。对比预训练模型与随机初始化模型，分析局部几何关系与草图级DOF状态的编码差异，并测量可解码信息在生成、干预和引导中的可行性。

Result: 1. 预训练大幅提升局部几何关系的线性可解码性，且在打乱顺序控制后优势依然存在。2. 草图级DOF状态在随机初始化表示中已高度可解码，预训练仅带来适度提升，表明其探针性能大多不依赖学习权重。3. 可解码信息不总是可行动的：生成常无法表达该信息；在两个接受干预测试的骨干网络中，修补实体位置的激活恢复效应消失，而可解码性跨层持续存在；均值差异引导也无法可靠控制输出。4. 可解码性、生成、激活层面影响、可引导性四者在测试设置中可能发散。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个受控审计框架，能区分模型未能编码几何结构与模型虽编码但未能表达或控制该信息的失败。结果揭示了LLMs内部表示的可解码性与实际行为能力之间的差距，提示仅依赖探针可解码性来推断模型“理解”可能产生误导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on structured reasoning tasks, but what they encode and whether it informs model behavior remain unclear. We investigate this question through geometric reasoning, using parametric CAD constraints as a controlled testbed for separating local pairwise relations from sketch-level constraint status. By probing the hidden states of six frozen decoder-only LLMs, we examine four properties: linear decodability, forced-choice generation, activation-level influence, and behavioral steerability. Pretraining substantially improves the decoding of local geometric relations, and this advantage persists after accounting for positional cues with shuffled-order controls. In contrast, sketch-level DOF status is already highly decodable from randomly initialized representations and improves only modestly with pretraining, indicating that much of its probe performance is available without learned weights. Further analyses show that decodable information is not always actionable. Generation often fails to express this information, and on the two intervention-tested backbones, activation-restoration effects at the patched entity position vanish while decodability persists across depth. Mean-difference steering also does not reliably control outputs. These results show that decodability, generation, activation-level influence, and steerability can diverge in the tested setting. The audit provides a controlled way to distinguish failures to encode geometric structure from failures to express or control encoded information.

</details>

*Franky Kevin Nando Tezoh,Ali Hussaini Umar,Alessandro Laio,Guido Sanguinetti,Riccardo Rende*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型（LLM）提示词重构问题，指出现有最小化困惑度的优化方法会产生不可读的伪提示词，将其归因于任务的不适定性。作者将提示词优化重构为贝叶斯后验推断问题，提出一种高效采样算法，能同时生成低困惑度且人类可读的提示词，在真实数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于困惑度最小化的提示词优化方法虽能引出目标回答，但常产生无人类可解释性的伪提示词（不可读的token串），限制了实际应用价值。作者认为这是提示词优化任务本身不适定导致的，需引入先验知识约束搜索空间。

Method: 将提示词重构建模为贝叶斯后验推断：以目标回答的似然为核心，引入提示词的先验分布（如语言模型先验）约束可读性，设计高效采样算法（如基于MCMC或变分推断）从后验分布中采样提示词，平衡困惑度与可读性。

Result: 在真实数据集实验中，所提方法在多个指标（如困惑度、人类可读性评分、任务完成率）上显著优于现有最优基线，生成的提示词既高效又具人类可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过贝叶斯重构解决提示词优化的不适定问题，提出的采样算法有效平衡了效率与可读性，为可解释的提示词工程提供了理论与实践框架。

Abstract: Reconstructing prompts that can elicit a desired answer or behaviour in an LLM is an open and important research topic. Optimisation methods which aim at minimising the perplexity of a given answer, however, consistently yield so-called pseudoprompts, unintelligible strings of tokens which can lack human interpretability. We argue that this is a consequence of the ill-posedness of the prompt optimisation task. By reframing the task as a Bayesian posterior inference over prompts, we propose an efficient algorithm to sample prompts which are both efficient (in terms of perplexity) and human readable. We compare our approach with state of the art alternatives showing on a real data set a marked improvement over a range of metrics.

</details>


### [37] [BEAR-Bench: A Bilingual Enterprise and Academic Reasoning Benchmark for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17895)
*Liubov Chubarova,Alexandra Kuleshova,Daniil Volkov,Kirill Sultanov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 BEAR-Bench，一个双语（英语和俄语）基准，包含 1000 个人工标注问题，基于文本密集的商务与科学文档，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在专业文档上的复杂推理能力。实验评估了 16 个专有和开放权重的 MLLMs，发现即使最强模型仍有显著提升空间，并利用模型输出对比现有幻觉检测方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注信息抽取、需要外部领域知识，或仅将专业文档作为众多场景之一，且以英语和中文为主，俄语等其他语言严重缺乏代表性。因此需要一个自包含、复杂、双语的基准，专门评估 MLLMs 在文本密集型专业文档上的推理能力。

Method: 构建 BEAR-Bench：收集商务和科学文档，人工标注 1000 个复杂推理问题，覆盖英语和俄语。选取 16 个代表性 MLLMs（包括 Gemini 3.1 Pro、Qwen3.5-397B 等）在该基准上进行评测。并利用模型输出，系统对比现有幻觉检测方法在识别模型失效方面的表现。

Result: 所有测试模型在 BEAR-Bench 上均表现出明显的性能上限（headroom），即使最强模型也未能达到满分。幻觉检测方法的对比显示，不同方法在识别模型失败方面的可靠性差异较大，为后续改进提供了依据。

Conclusion: BEAR-Bench 填补了多语言、文本密集型专业文档复杂推理评测的空白，揭示了当前 MLLMs 在该任务上的不足，并为幻觉检测研究提供了新的评测视角。未来工作可致力于提升模型在专业文档上的推理能力，并开发更可靠的幻觉检测机制。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made significant strides in visual comprehension, their ability to reason about text-dense, professional documents remains incompletely evaluated. Existing benchmarks emphasize information extraction, require external domain knowledge, or cover professional documents only as one of many settings. They are also largely English- or Chinese-centric, leaving other languages and Russian, in particular, substantially underrepresented. To address these limitations, we introduce BEAR-Bench (Bilingual Enterprise and Academic Reasoning), a self-contained, complex English-and-Russian benchmark comprising 1000 human-annotated questions based on text-rich business and scientific documents. We evaluate 16 proprietary and open-weight MLLMs, including Gemini 3.1 Pro and Qwen3.5-397B, on BEAR-Bench and observe clear headroom even for the strongest systems. Finally, we use the resulting model outputs to compare existing hallucination detection methods, evaluating not only how often models fail on BEAR-Bench but also how reliably those failures can be identified.

</details>


### [38] [CABLE: Extending the Reach of Memory Retrieval via Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17911)
*Zheling Tan,Jin Gao,Dequan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 CABLE (Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion)，一种即插即用的增强模块，通过构建稀疏的、与宿主检索器互补的前因导向链接，解决长期对话记忆中语义相似度检索无法触达语义距离较远但推理相关的历史证据的问题。实验表明 CABLE 在多个记忆系统和基准上均显著提升 LLM 判分，尤其在跨会话、开放域和偏好类问题上增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期对话记忆系统主要依赖语义相似度检索，擅长主题回溯，但难以找回与当前事件语义距离较远却能解释其动机、计划或前因的早期经验。现有记忆图虽提供跨记忆结构，但其基于语义重叠的链接往往重复了宿主检索器已能覆盖的内容，未能提供互补的推理线索。

Method: CABLE 为每条新记忆生成前因导向查询，检索候选先前记忆，减去直接语义邻域内的候选，验证剩余候选后将其作为互补关联加入稀疏有向图。检索时，CABLE 沿这些链接扩展宿主系统的种子记忆，挖掘隐性支撑证据。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 MA-LongMemEval 基准上，CABLE 分别与 A-MEM、SimpleMem、Mem0g 集成，使用 Qwen3.5-27B、DeepSeek-chat、GPT-4o-mini 评测。所有系统级设置下 CABLE 均获得更高的平均 LLM 判分，在证据分散于多记忆或多会话的开放域、多会话、偏好导向问题类别中增益最显著。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持在长期对话记忆中优先构建稀疏的、推理相关的、与宿主检索器互补而非重复的关联链接，从而有效拓展检索器的直接语义触达范围，提升复杂问答中的证据回溯能力。

Abstract: As LLM agents operate across structured workflows and sessions, preserving long-term history does not ensure that later contexts can recover relevant evidence through a bounded memory interface. We study this evidence-reachability problem in long-term conversational memory, where retrieval still relies heavily on semantic similarity. This works well for topical recall, but it often misses earlier experiences, plans, or motivations that are semantically distant from the later events they help explain. Existing memory graphs provide cross-memory structure, yet links driven mainly by semantic overlap can duplicate what the host retriever already recovers. We argue that link construction should instead prioritize a sparse set of retriever-complementary associations. We present CABLE (Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion), a plug-in augmentation that constructs links designed to extend the host retriever's direct semantic reach. For each new memory, CABLE generates antecedent-oriented queries, retrieves prior memories, subtracts candidates in the direct semantic neighborhood, and verifies the remainder before adding the accepted complementary associations into a sparse directed graph. At retrieval time, CABLE expands the host system's retrieved seeds along these links to surface implicit supporting evidence. We evaluate CABLE with A-MEM on LoCoMo and MA-LongMemEval, and further integrate it into SimpleMem and Mem0g on LoCoMo, using Qwen3.5-27B, DeepSeek-chat, and GPT-4o-mini. CABLE yields higher mean LLM-judge scores in every evaluated system-level setting, with the largest gains in categories where useful evidence is distributed across memories or sessions, including open-domain, multi-session, and preference-oriented questions. These results support prioritizing sparse, reasoning-relevant associations that complement rather than duplicate the host retriever.

</details>


### [39] [SpeechSense: A Paralinguistic-Focused Dataset for Fine-Grained Speech Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17931)
*Shicheng Ma,Wenqian Cui,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为SpeechSense的新数据集，用于细粒度语音情感分析，定义了8类主要通过韵律线索可检测的人际立场分类体系，并通过高保真语音合成和人工验证构建数据集。实验表明，具有声学访问权限的模型显著优于纯文本基线，验证了声学线索在检测细微说话人态度中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音情感分析研究存在两大局限：1) 主流方法依赖以文本为中心的级联流程（ASR+文本分析），不可避免地丢弃了韵律、语调等关键声学特征，无法捕捉声学歧义话语中的态度含义；2) 现有基准测试标签粒度不匹配，优先考虑基本情绪（如快乐、悲伤），而忽视了社会敏感场景所需的细微人际立场（如自信、不耐烦）。

Method: 1) 定义了一个包含8类人际立场的专用分类体系，这些立场主要可通过超越词汇内容的韵律线索检测；2) 基于该分类体系，利用高保真语音合成技术并结合严格的人工验证，构建了SpeechSense数据集；3) 在多模态LLM、纯文本LLM和语音编码器上进行全面实验对比。

Result: 跨多模态LLM、纯文本LLM和语音编码器的综合实验表明，拥有声学访问权限的模型在检测细微说话人态度方面始终优于纯文本基线，实证验证了声学线索的首要地位。

Conclusion: SpeechSense数据集填补了细粒度语音情感分析中声学线索建模的空白，实验结果强调了在社交敏感应用中整合声学信息的必要性，为未来语音理解研究提供了重要基准资源。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI have revolutionized speech processing, yet effective speech understanding requires discerning not just what is said, but how it is said. Speech Sentiment Analysis plays a critical role in decoding these paralinguistic cues for diverse real-world applications such as recruitment and customer service. However, existing Speech Sentiment Analysis research faces two primary limitations. First, dominant approaches rely on text-centric pipelines that cascade Automatic Speech Recognition with text analysis. This process inevitably discards essential acoustic features like prosody and tone, failing to capture attitudinal meanings in acoustically ambiguous utterances. Second, current benchmarks suffer from a mismatch in label granularity, prioritizing basic emotions (e.g., happy, sad) over the nuanced interpersonal stances (e.g., confident, impatient) necessary for social sensitivity. To address these limitations, we propose a novel dataset, SpeechSense, for fine-grained speech sentiment analysis. Specifically, we define a specialized 8-class taxonomy of interpersonal stances detectable primarily through prosodic cues beyond lexical content alone. We then construct a curated dataset based on this taxonomy, built from high-fidelity speech synthesis and rigorous human validation. Comprehensive experiments across multi-modal LLMs, text-only LLMs, and speech encoders demonstrate that models with acoustic access consistently outperform text-only baselines. These results empirically validate the primacy of acoustic cues in detecting subtle speaker attitudes, highlighting the necessity of SpeechSense. Dataset and supplementary materials are available at https://github.com/Sher13cked/SpeechSense.

</details>


### [40] [Grading Needs a Rubric, Not Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17938)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 小语言模型在使用明确评分细则时，能像大型昂贵模型一样可靠地评分开放式考试答案。研究提出any-to-bench设计：前沿模型一次性提取题目和评分细则，低成本模型负责重复评分。实验表明，答案本身解释了95.6%的分数方差，评分者身份仅占0.2%。评分细则中的标准答案是解耦评分与评分者智能的关键。无长度偏好或同家族偏好证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证小模型在有明确评分细则时能否以低成本可靠地完成开放式考试评分，并探究评分细则中哪些成分起关键作用，以及评分者模型的推理努力对评分的影响。

Method: 提出any-to-bench流程：前沿模型一次性读取源文档提取题目与评分细则；六种低成本模型配置（两模型家族×三推理努力级别）各答24道开放式题，并对每份答案评分三次，共得3,456个单题评分。引入六个前沿级评分者作对照。做两次消融：先去除评分细则的标准与等级但保留标准答案，再连标准答案一并去除，观察可靠性（ICC）、分数分布及评分者推理努力的影响变化。

Result: 答案身份解释95.6%分数方差，评分者身份仅0.2%。写作者推理努力提升可使得分变动0.143满分，评分者推理努力影响≤0.006。前沿评分者复现相同分数，组成评分组也无更高可靠性。仅保留标准答案时可靠性不变；去除标准答案后ICC从0.888跌至0.628，分数膨胀，评分者推理努力重新显著。未发现长度偏好或同家族偏好。

Conclusion: 评分细则（特别是标准答案）能将评分任务与评分者模型智能解耦，使小模型也能高可靠评分。any-to-bench范式可大幅降低大规模开放式评分成本。

Abstract: Small language models can grade open-ended examination answers as reliably as substantially more expensive models when they grade against an explicit rubric. We test this claim as the design principle behind any-to-bench: a frontier model reads source documents once, at ingestion, to extract each question and its rubric; lower-cost models then perform all repeated grading work. We evaluate six cost-efficient model configurations from two model families at three reasoning-effort levels. Each configuration answers 24 open-ended examination questions, and each also grades every answer sheet three times, yielding 3,456 per-question grades. Scores depend overwhelmingly on the answer being graded: answer identity explains 95.6% of score variance, whereas judge identity explains only 0.2%. Raising a writer's reasoning effort moves earned scores by as much as 0.143 of full marks, while raising a judge's reasoning effort moves assigned scores by at most 0.006. Six frontier-tier judges, added as a check, reproduce these scores and are no more reliable as a panel. Two ablations then decompose the rubric on the same questions and answers. Removing its criteria and levels while keeping the official answer changes nothing measurable. Removing the official answer as well collapses reliability (ICC 0.888 to 0.628), inflates scores, and makes judge reasoning effort matter again. The rubric is what decouples grading from judge intelligence, and within the rubric the official answer does nearly all the work. We find no evidence of length preference or same-family preference under rubric-anchored grading.

</details>


### [41] [Do Large Language Models Play Six Degrees of Separation? Measuring Topological Compression in Long-Context Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17950)
*Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过分析大语言模型隐藏状态流形的动态几何结构，发现深层潜在空间自发组织成小世界网络，揭示了多跳推理的拓扑相变机制，并利用此几何特征实现了零样本幻觉检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力的可解释性方法难以捕捉真实语义邻近性，因注意力汇聚等路由伪影导致失效；深层推理机制尚不明确，需要绕过注意力权重直接研究隐藏状态流形的几何拓扑结构。

Method: 将长上下文表示的连续相似度矩阵稀疏化为无权图，分析两种不同架构中高度不相连语义锚点间的连通性，追踪隐藏状态流形的动态几何演化。

Result: 发现早期句法层完全破碎，深层推理层突发性地将巨大概念距离压缩为高度可导航的路径，严格受限于“六度分隔”上限（≤6语义跳跃）；事实生成与源上下文保持结构完整（约3跳），幻觉导致严重拓扑坍缩。

Conclusion: 该工作从数学上形式化了Transformer执行抽象推理的机制，提供了一个纯几何的签名用于评估事实可靠性，为零样本幻觉检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable multi-hop reasoning capabilities over long contexts, yet the internal mechanisms enabling these distant cognitive leaps remain poorly understood. Traditional attention-based interpretability often fails to capture true semantic proximity due to routing artifacts like attention sinks. In this paper, we bypass attention weights to directly analyze the dynamic geometry of the hidden state manifold, proving that deep LLM latent spaces natively organize into Small-World networks. By sparsifying the continuous similarity matrices of long-context representations into unweighted graphs, we trace the connectivity between highly disjoint semantic anchors across two distinct architectures. Our findings reveal a sharp topological phase transition: while early syntactic layers remain entirely fractured, deep reasoning layers abruptly compress massive conceptual distances into highly navigable pathways strictly bounded by the "Six Degrees of Separation" limit (=< 6 semantic hops). Furthermore, we demonstrate the practical efficacy of this framework by applying it to zero-shot hallucination detection within Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) using the RAGognize dataset. We show that factually grounded generations maintain structural integrity with their source context (approximately 3 hops), whereas hallucinations induce severe topological collapse. Ultimately, this work mathematically formalizes how transformers execute abstract reasoning and provides a novel, strictly geometric signature for evaluating factual reliability.

</details>


### [42] [When Writing Style Drifts: Benchmarking Authorship Verification under Distribution Shifts in Genre, Time and the AI-Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17979)
*Lotta Kiefer,Brisca Balthes,Christoph Leiter,Yamen Ajjour,Elena Schmidt,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AVShift，首个德语作者身份验证基准，系统评估跨体裁、时间跨度及 AI 时代分布偏移对模型鲁棒性的影响。实验表明：微调 LLM 在跨体裁泛化最优，且受益于文体多样训练数据；时间漂移是最强影响因素，性能随时间间隔显著下降；未观测到可测度的 AI 时代分布偏移；部分文体特征跨体裁稳定，但相对重要性随体裁转换变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作者身份验证基准多孤立研究体裁、时间、AI 写作等因素，且以英语为主，难以反映真实场景下的多重分布偏移。

Method: 构建包含 15 万+ 文本对的德语基准 AVShift，覆盖三种体裁、21 年跨度，统一框架下受控评估跨体裁、时间、AI 时代偏移。对比特征型、嵌入型、LLM 型代表性方法。

Result: 微调 LLM 跨体裁泛化最佳，文体多样训练数据显著提升鲁棒性；时间漂移为最强影响因素，性能随时间间隔增大显著下降；AVShift 内未发现可测度的 AI 时代分布偏移；文体特征跨体裁稳定，但相对重要性随体裁转换变化。

Conclusion: AVShift 为多重分布偏移下的作者身份验证提供了首个德语统一评测平台，揭示时间漂移比 AI 写作影响更大，并指出文体特征稳定性与体裁相关，为鲁棒模型设计提供实证指导。

Abstract: Authorship verification (AV) assumes that an author's writing style remains sufficiently stable to distinguish it from that of other writers. In practice, however, this assumption is challenged by distribution shifts caused by changes in genre, time, and AI-assisted writing. Existing AV benchmarks typically study these factors in isolation and focus predominantly on English, limiting our understanding of model robustness under realistic conditions. We introduce AVShift, the first German benchmark for systematically evaluating AV under multiple distribution shifts. AVShift comprises over 150,000 text pairs spanning three genres and 21 years, enabling controlled evaluation of cross-genre, temporal, and AI-era shifts within a unified framework. We benchmark representative feature-based, embedding-based, and LLM-based approaches. Our experiments show that fine-tuned LLMs generalize best across genres and benefit substantially from stylistically diverse training data. We further demonstrate that temporal drift is one of the strongest factors affecting AV, with performance degrading significantly as the time gap between documents increases. In contrast, we find no evidence of a measurable AI-era distribution shift within AVShift. Finally, our feature analysis reveals stylistic features that remain stable across genres, while their relative importance varies depending on the specific genre transition. We release AVShift and our code for future research.

</details>


### [43] [Judge, Retrieve, or Abstain: Uncertainty-Guarded LLM Judging with Provable Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17994)
*Sher Badshah,Ali Emami,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种风险控制框架，用于LLM作为裁判员评估客观任务时的事实正确性。通过在保留集上校准不确定性阈值，利用Clopper-Pearson区间控制虚假发现率（FDR）在用户指定水平α以下，实现有限样本保证。采用双阈值路由机制：参数化模式置信度不足时路由到检索增强模式，在第二个校准阈值下重新评估。在开放域QA基准上，该框架在保持目标错误率的同时显著提高了覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用LLM作为裁判员评估客观任务（如事实正确性）时面临可靠性挑战：参数化知识可能产生幻觉或证据不足；工具增强虽提供额外证据但引入计算成本，且缺乏何时、如何可靠使用证据的机制。更重要的是，现有方法无法提供对接受裁决风险的正式控制，也无法保证在指定水平上的可靠性。

Method: 提出风险控制框架：1) 在保留集上校准不确定性阈值，使用有限样本Clopper-Pearson区间，使接受裁决中的虚假发现率（FDR）以高概率低于用户指定的α；2) 双阈值路由机制：首先使用参数化模式评估，若置信度不足（不确定性超阈值），则路由到检索增强模式，裁判员收集网络证据后在第二个校准阈值下重新评估；3) 有限样本保证无需额外假设即可延伸至双阈值路由。

Result: 在开放域QA基准及不同规模的裁判员模型上，该框架始终维持目标错误率（FDR ≤ α），同时相比单模式基线（仅参数化或仅检索增强）实现了实质性更高的覆盖率（即接受更多有效裁决）。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM裁判员在客观任务上的可靠评估提供了首个具有有限样本统计保证的风险控制框架。双阈值路由机制有效平衡了效率与可靠性，在保证错误率受控的前提下最大化覆盖率，为大规模部署LLM裁判员提供了理论与实践基础。

Abstract: Using LLMs as judges has become standard practice for evaluating model outputs at scale. This is particularly common for subjective, open-ended tasks such as assessing helpfulness or alignment, where no single reference answer exists. However, objective tasks introduce a distinct reliability challenge for reference-free LLM judging. In the absence of a reference answer, the judge evaluates factual correctness either through its parametric knowledge or through tool augmentation. Although the former enables efficient evaluation, the judge may hallucinate or lack sufficient evidence for its verdict. Conversely, tool augmentation can provide additional evidence but introduces extra computational cost and requires an appropriate mechanism to determine when and how that evidence should be used reliably. More importantly, neither approach alone provides formal control over the risk of accepted verdicts or guarantees their reliability at a specified level. We propose a risk-controlled framework that calibrates uncertainty thresholds on a held-out set so that the false discovery rate among accepted verdicts remains below a user-specified level~$α$ with high probability, using finite-sample Clopper--Pearson intervals. When the parametric mode is not sufficiently confident, the instance is routed to a retrieval-augmented mode, where the judge gathers web evidence and re-evaluates the instance under a second calibrated threshold. The finite-sample guarantee carries over to this two-threshold routing without additional assumptions. Across open-domain QA benchmarks and judges of varying scales, the framework maintains the target error rate while achieving substantially higher coverage than single-mode baselines.

</details>


### [44] [The IOL-AI Challenge: An Open Challenge towards Advancing Linguistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18011)
*Eduardo Sánchez,Rita Berrada,Dan-Mircea Mirea,Sara Rajaee,Alexander Piperski,Ana Meta Dolinar,Boris Iomdin,Andrey Nikulin,Mariya Shmatova,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍IOL-AI挑战赛，评估大语言模型在语言学推理任务上的表现，发现前沿模型Claude Opus 4.8可获金牌级成绩，但资源受限系统表现差；模型规模非决定因素，解码和输出处理更关键；自动指标与专家评分一致但压缩量表；语言学推理是泛化推理能力的强基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理研究多集中于数学和代码等有明确规则的领域，而语言学谜题要求解题者先发现系统再在其中推理，因此提出IOL-AI挑战赛，利用国际语言学奥林匹克竞赛题目评估模型的语言推理能力。

Method: 开放科学竞赛，使用IOL 2026个人赛未见题目，自动评估和官方IOL评委按人类评分标准评分；731个提交，46支队伍，严格算力预算（一张T4，30分钟）；额外基准测试15个无约束前沿和开源模型。

Result: Claude Opus 4.8获评委评分相当于金牌；资源受限系统得分处于参赛者后5%；14B模型提交优于双倍大小模型；增益来自解码和输出处理而非模型容量；自动指标排名与评委一致但压缩量表，弱系统高估约13分，强系统低估；模型对题目语言的先验知识不显著帮助解题。

Conclusion: 语言学推理作为泛化推理技能的强基准，前沿模型虽可能拥有部分题目语言的先验知识，但并不显著提升解题能力，说明语言推理任务能有效测试模型的泛化推理能力。

Abstract: Reasoning in LLMs is overwhelmingly studied in domains that provide a model with rules: mathematics and code. Linguistic puzzles invert this: the solver must first discover the system before reasoning within it. We present the IOL-AI Challenge, an open-science competition run on the unseen problems of the International Linguistics Olympiad (IOL) 2026 Individual Contest, evaluated both automatically and, for the first time, by members of the official IOL Jury under the same rubrics applied to human contestants. The challenge drew 731 submissions from 46 teams under a strict compute budget (one T4, 30 mins). We additionally benchmark 15 unconstrained frontier and open models, with Claude Opus 4.8 earning a jury score equivalent to a gold medal, while both resource-constrained systems we submitted for jury grading scored in the range of the bottom 5% of contestants. Capability was not determined by scale: 14B submissions outperform models twice their size, and gains come from decoding and output-handling rather than model capacity. We also found that automatic metrics rank systems exactly as the jury does, but compress the scale, upscoring weak systems by ~13 points and understating strong ones. Our analysis shows that while frontier models might have prior knowledge about some of the problem languages, it does not significantly help them solve the linguistic reasoning tasks, leaving linguistic reasoning as a strong benchmarking proxy for generalizable reasoning skills.

</details>


### [45] [Chain-of-Experience for Continual LLM Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18027)
*Haoqin Tu,Yunhao Fang,Yizhong Wang,Cihang Xie,Shen Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain-of-Experience (CoE)框架，使LLM在测试时通过迭代交互与反馈（自我反馈、环境信号如正确性、代码测试通过率）积累经验并持续改进。在数学、编程、知识三大领域对8个顶尖模型（含GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro、Claude-4.5 Sonnet）实验表明：CoE显著优于无反馈基线，整体提升5.6%且API成本降19%；多反馈通道互补增益明显；单位token准确率超越现有测试时策略；基础能力强的模型改进潜力更大；对弱/虚假反馈鲁棒；多数增益出现在早期迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估忽略模型在推理时通过交互从经验中学习并持续改进的能力，而人类正是通过持续经验学习不断进步。

Method: 提出Chain-of-Experience (CoE)范式：模型通过多轮迭代与自我反馈或环境反馈（正确性判定、公开编程测试通过率等）交互，积累经验轨迹形成持续改进闭环。在数学、编程、知识三领域，对8个LLM（含GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro、Claude-4.5 Sonnet）进行系统评测。

Result: 1) 迭代经验持续优于无反馈基线，仅靠自我反馈即获显著增益；2) 总体准确率提升5.6%，API成本降低19%；3) 互补反馈通道（如模型反馈+正确性信号）组合带来额外增益；4) 单位token准确率超越现有测试时策略；5) 基础能力与改进容量呈正相关；6) 对弱/虚假反馈保持鲁棒；7) 不同反馈贡献不同改进维度；8) 大部分增益集中在早期迭代。

Conclusion: LLM具备在测试时通过迭代经验学习实现持续自我改进的能力，CoE框架以更低成本、更高token效率实现了超越零样本推理的持续性能提升，为测试时计算扩展提供了新范式。

Abstract: Humans continuously learn from experience, whereas conventional large language model (LLM) evaluations ignore the models' ability to improve through inference-time interaction. In this paper, we study how LLMs learn from iterative experience at test time, a setting we refer to as Chain-of-Experience (CoE), where models accumulate experiential traces through iterative interactions with self or environmental feedback to form a continual improvement loop beyond zero-shot inference. We instantiate CoE with diverse feedback mechanisms, including model self-feedback and environmental signals such as correctness or public coding test pass rates, and evaluate across math, coding, and knowledge domains using 8 LLMs, including GPT-5, Gemini-2.5 Pro, Claude-4.5 Sonnet. Our study shows that leveraging iterative experience consistently outperforms feedback-free baselines, achieving substantial gains with self feedback alone, alongside a 5.6% overall improvement and 19% lower API cost across tasks and models. We further show that combining complementary feedback channels (e.g., model and correctness signals) yields additional gains, and that CoE delivers higher accuracy per token than existing test-time strategies. We observe a positive correlation between LLM base ability and improvement capacity, and show that models remain robust under weak or spurious feedback, with different feedback contributing to distinct improvement aspects and most gains emerging early in the iterations.

</details>


### [46] [Language Has Two Parameters: Narrative-Induced Semantic Plasticity and Phase-Sensitive Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18041)
*Hollis Robbins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出语言具有两个参数：振幅（共现强度）和相位（意义间的关系参数）。标准语料库统计和Transformer模型仅捕捉振幅，忽略了相位——它决定共激活意义如何组合，能反转意义贡献而不改变意义本身。相位依赖于个体/双人交互历史，群体平均会抹除它。论文给出弱版主张（解释需要第二参数）和强版主张（量子概率约束现象），并提出六个可检验预测，呼吁构建带有代理索引相位语义状态的语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有词嵌入和注意力机制仅基于语料库共现统计（振幅），无法捕捉意义组合中依赖于个体交互历史、可逆转贡献的关系参数（相位）。群体去个性化的语料库丢失了相位信息，导致标准Transformer在冻结推理中缺乏显式相位表征，且可解释性研究将共存视为缺陷，实则是典引、反讽、引用的条件。

Method: 理论分析与形式化建模：引入量子概率作为相位参数的标记法（不主张大脑量子过程），区分弱版（需第二关系参数）和强版（量子演算约束现象）主张；提出六个可检验预测（抑制意义是否保持激活、遭遇顺序影响、标记改变理解、历史是否改变模型而非仅告知）；推导出所需架构：代理索引、携带相位的语义状态语言模型。

Result: 论证了相位参数的必要性：它是有符号的、持久的、索引于个体/双人；标准Transformer缺乏显式相位表征；可解释性追求单义性实则优化掉了相位。六个预测为实证检验提供了路径。强版主张依赖于尚未推导出的遭遇顺序约束。

Conclusion: 语言理解需要超越振幅的第二参数——相位。未来架构应构建代理索引、相位承载的语义状态模型。量子概率提供合适的数学标记法，但强版主张需进一步推导遭遇顺序约束。这重新定义了语言模型中意义表征与组合的理论基础。

Abstract: Language has two parameters. Count how often words occur together and you estimate amplitude, the strength of association. Word embeddings and attention weights refine that count, which sums every writer in the corpus together. This paper claims a second parameter, phase, which signed weights learned from a corpus do not supply. Phase exists only between meanings: it determines how coactivated meanings combine, and it can reverse what a meaning contributes while that meaning stays fully present. A speaker can set phase in the signal through linguistic form; encounters install phase relations and history distributes them. Population averaging deletes history-indexed phase: agent-deindexed corpora identify the population marginal state and determine no individual or dyadic state, at any scale. The standard transformer has no explicit representation for phase in frozen inference, and the interpretability program measuring progress by monosemanticity is optimizing against it: the coexistence it treats as a defect is the condition of allusion, irony, and quotation. Six predictions test whether a suppressed meaning stays active, whether encounter order changes what a phrase does, whether marking the signal changes how a shared phrase is taken, and whether a model given a history is changed by it or only informed about it. The claim defended is the weak version: interpretation requires a second relational parameter, signed, persistent, and indexed to individuals and dyads. Quantum probability is one notation for the parameter; nothing in the formalism claims quantum processes in the brain. The strong version, that the quantum calculus constrains these phenomena as signed classical models do not, rests on an encounter-order constraint not yet derived. The architecture the theory calls for is a language model with agent-indexed, phase-bearing semantic states.

</details>


### [47] [TokEval: A Tokenizer Evaluation Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18062)
*Clara Meister*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TokEval框架，通过内在度量指标预测下游模型性能，发现信息论指标预测语言建模能力，结构敏感指标关联任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前tokenizer选择缺乏充分评估，设计选择直接影响模型能力，但对哪些tokenizer属性影响哪些下游性能理解有限。

Method: 引入TokEval框架，包含语言学和结构意义的指标（如UTF-8字符边界完整性、数字位值边界对齐）。通过受控语言模型预训练实验，仅改变tokenizer的训练数据混合、预分词策略、训练算法，在bits-per-byte和多个基准（语言理解、数学推理、代码生成）上评估。

Result: 实验表明不同内在属性对模型能力影响不同：信息论指标预测语言建模能力（Spearman rho高达0.80），结构敏感指标（如数字和换行处理）与任务准确率相关。

Conclusion: TokEval可实现更原则化的tokenizer评估，在内在度量与预训练结果一致时，可用内在度量替代预训练扫描。

Abstract: Language model tokenizers are typically selected with minimal evaluation, despite the fact that their design choices directly impact model capabilities. This can be partly attributed to a limited understanding of which tokenizer properties affect which aspects of downstream performance. We introduce TokEval, a framework of tokenizer evaluation metrics that goes beyond standard measures like fertility and compression rate to capture linguistically and structurally meaningful properties, e.g., UTF-8 character boundary integrity and digit place-value boundary alignment for mathematics. To validate whether these metrics are predictive of downstream model performance, we conduct controlled language model pretraining experiments, varying solely the tokenizers' training data mixture, pretokenization strategy, and training algorithm. We evaluate the resulting models on bits-per-byte (a tokenizer-agnostic version of perplexity) and several benchmarks, spanning linguistic understanding, mathematical reasoning, and code generation. Our experiments suggest that different intrinsic properties have different impacts on model abilities: information-theoretic metrics predict language modeling abilities (Spearman rho up to 0.80), while structure-sensitive metrics, such as those measuring digit and line-break handling, correlate with task accuracy. We hope TokEval enables more principled tokenizer evaluation, replacing pretraining sweeps with intrinsic measurement wherever the two agree.

</details>


### [48] [Multi-Agent AI System for Radiology Report Structuring and Quality Assurance with Independent Radiologist Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18072)
*Iryna Hartsock,Cesar Lam,Christopher Otteni,Aliya Qayyum,Robert Gatenby,Cyrillo Araujo,Ghulam Rasool*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究开发并评估了一个本地部署的多智能体AI系统，用于放射学报告的结构化和质量保证。系统将638份CT报告按解剖区域结构化，并检测发现与印象不一致、性别-解剖冲突及关键发现未记录等问题。放射科医生评估显示系统结构化准确率69%，质量保证表现84%为优秀或良好，支持报告标准化和质控流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决放射学报告结构化不足、质量保证依赖人工审核效率低且易遗漏错误的问题，旨在开发一个可本地部署、集成结构化与质控的自动化系统，提升报告标准化水平和患者安全。

Method: 回顾性研究纳入15名放射科医生撰写的638份胸腹盆CT报告。构建多智能体AI流水线：利用正则表达式规则和本地大语言模型将发现部分按句子级别映射到预定义解剖章节；同时部署质检智能体检测发现-印象不一致、章节内不一致、性别-解剖冲突及关键发现未沟通记录。两名资深放射科医生独立评估45份报告子集的结构化准确性和质控表现。

Result: 系统成功结构化所有报告的发现部分（22,270句），标记90份报告（14.1%）存在问题，以章节不匹配最多（80份，12.5%）。放射科医生评估：双方一致认为31份（69%）结构化正确，2份（4%）错误，12份（27%）有分歧；均同意无临床重要信息遗漏或捏造内容；84%报告质控评价为优秀或良好，其余为可接受。

Conclusion: 本地部署的多智能体AI系统在单一工作流中实现了放射报告结构化与质量保证的结合，经放射科医生评估表现良好，有望支持放射科报告标准化和质量保证实践。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a locally deployed multi-agent AI system for radiology report structuring and quality assurance. Materials and Methods: This retrospective study included 638 radiology reports from CT examinations of the chest, abdomen, and pelvis dictated by 15 board-certified radiologists in 2023 and 2024. A multi-agent AI pipeline was developed to perform report structuring and quality assurance (QA). The system structured the report into standardized anatomical sections at the sentence level using regex rules and local large language models. It also detected mismatches between the Findings and Impression sections, or within sections; gender-anatomy conflicts; and undocumented communication of critical findings. Two board-certified radiologists independently evaluated a 45-report subset. Results: The multi-agent system structured the Findings sections of all reports (22,270 sentences) into a predefined anatomical format while retaining the original report content. The system flagged 90 (14.1%) reports, most commonly for section mismatches (80 reports, 12.5%). In the radiologist evaluation, both reviewers agreed that 31 (69%) were correctly restructured, 2 reports (4%) were incorrectly restructured, and disagreed on the remaining 12 reports (27%). Both reviewers agreed that no clinically important information was omitted and no fabricated content was introduced. Overall QA performance was rated as "excellent" or "good" in 84% of the evaluated reports, with the remaining reports rated as "fair". Conclusion: A locally deployed multi-agent AI system combined radiology report structuring and quality assurance within a single workflow. The system demonstrated favorable performance in radiologist evaluation. Such systems may support standardization of reporting and quality assurance in radiology practice.

</details>


### [49] [Margin-Regularized Structured Semantic Alignment for Brain-Language Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16975)
*Jiaqi Wang,Huawen Hu,Shu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出MD-SigLIP框架，通过边际正则化的结构化语义对齐，将脑嵌入与文本嵌入在共享语义空间对齐，实现基于检索的解码，显式建模神经表征与语言语义的对应关系，并在全词汇表和子集评估中达到最优检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型驱动的脑语言解码虽取得进展，但不清楚解码内容是真实反映神经表征还是主要由语言模型重建，限制了可解释性和对内在脑语言对应关系的研究。

Method: 提出MD-SigLIP，基于重复感知的Sigmoid对比学习，引入列表式边际正则化项，强制正语义簇与负样本间的结构化排序约束，同时建模多正样本语义结构和基于边际的排序，捕捉神经信号中反映的语言嵌入流形组织。

Result: 实验在全词汇表和子集评估设置下均展示了最先进的检索性能。

Conclusion: MD-SigLIP通过显式对齐脑嵌入与文本嵌入并建模结构化语义关系，有效解决了脑语言解码中的歧义问题，提升了可解释性和脑语言内在对应关系的探究能力。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models, brain-language decoding has achieved remarkable progress. However, it remains unclear whether decoded content genuinely reflects neural representations or is largely reconstructed by the language model itself. This ambiguity limits interpretability and hinders the investigation of intrinsic brain-language correspondence. To address this challenge, we propose MD-SigLIP. This margin-regularized structured semantic alignment framework directly aligns brain embeddings with text embeddings in a shared semantic space, enabling retrieval-based decoding. This formulation enables explicit modeling of the correspondence between neural representations and language semantics. Building upon duplicate-aware sigmoid contrastive learning, we introduce a listwise margin-regularized term that enforces structured ranking constraints between positive semantic clusters and negative samples. By modeling multi-positive semantic structure and margin-based ordering simultaneously, the method captures the manifold organization of language embeddings reflected in neural signals. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art retrieval performance under both full-vocabulary and subset evaluation settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [50] [Detecting and Discriminating Operator Misspecification in Hybrid PDE-Parameter Learning: a Reference-Free Instrument, with Discrimination Bounded In Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16925)
*Eric Fock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种诊断工具，能从单次拟合中检测混合PDE-参数估计中的算子设定错误，并将其与参数不可辨识性区分开来。实验表明该工具有效分离了这两种失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的精度检查（如RMSE）对算子设定错误不敏感：设定错误的估计器域内RMSE很低（低于观测噪声），但系数偏差却高达约30%。同时需要区分设定错误与参数不可辨识性，因为单纯的检测已是拥挤领域。

Method: 构建基于信息矩阵的统计量（带插入式尺度和逐种子参数），无需神谕即可从单次拟合计算。在自伴抛物型反问题上测试，预先注册拒绝上限和边界。对比正确设定、两种设定错误、不可辨识但正确设定三种设计。同时考察一参数曲线拟合、裸参数、不同规模MLP及物理信息网络等估计器。

Result: 正确设定下统计量中位数0.19，拒绝率0.033（低于上限0.10）；两种设定错误下统计量跃升至224和85，每次复现均触发。不可辨识设计下统计量保持低值（0.050），而秩统计量在预注册边界处坍塌为零。两种读数分离了三种设计中的两类失效。设定错误估计器的域内RMSE为2.7e-2（低于σ≥0.05的观测噪声），但系数错误29.7%（零噪声）至31.2%（最大噪声）。各架构收敛到同一伪真值（闭式匹配0.07%），唯独物理信息网络因复合目标收敛到不相交的伪真值。报告了工具失效案例、神经估计器不及Tikhonov正则化的预注册负面结果，以及保证成立但训练网络违反的假设。

Conclusion: 该工具提供了一种可部署的检验，能将算子设定错误与参数不可辨识性分开——这是单纯检测无法做到的。样本内给出界限，样本外给出方向。因为常规精度检查对设定错误是盲目的，所以这种分离是必要的。

Abstract: We build an instrument that reads, from a single fit and with no oracle, whether the operator a hybrid PDE-parameter estimator postulates is wrong-and separates that from a merely unidentifiable parameter. On one self-adjoint parabolic inverse problem, an information-matrix statistic with plug-in scale and per-seed parameter has median 0.19 under correct specification, rejection rate $0.033$ against a pre-registered ceiling of $0.10$, and rises to $224$ and $85$ under two misspecifications, firing in every replicate. On a correctly specified but non-identifiable design it stays mute-$0.050$ at $n=200$, Clopper-Pearson $[0.024, 0.090]$-while a rank statistic collapses to zero at a pre-registered boundary $c_5^*=2.15\times10^{-3}.$ Two readings of one fit therefore separate the two failures across the three designs a deployable test reaches. That separation is the contribution; detection alone is a crowded flank. In sample it is a bound, out of sample a direction. It is needed because the usual accuracy check is blind: the misspecified estimator's in-domain RMSE is $2.7\times 10^{-2}$, below the observation noise for $σ\geq 0.05,$ while the coefficient is wrong by $29.7\%$ at zero noise, $31.2\%$ at the loudest. Nor is the failure architectural: a one-parameter curve fit, a bare parameter and multilayer perceptrons of $49$ and $241$ parameters converge to the same pseudo-true, matched in closed form to $0.07\%,$ whereas a physics-informed network, with its composite objective, converges to a disjoint one. We report where the instrument is blind, a pre-registered negative where a neural estimator loses to Tikhonov-regularized inversion at recovery, and the hypothesis under which its guarantee holds but a trained network violates it.

</details>


### [51] [Data-DPO: Direct Preference Optimization for Target Model Data Selection in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16926)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Data-DPO是一种面向目标模型的SFT数据选择方法，通过单步探测观察目标模型在不同样本上的局部训练反馈，将样本间激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量级奖励模型学习目标模型感知的数据偏好，并结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边际多样性构建更稳定有效的训练子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法通常将数据价值视为相对静态的属性，较少关注数据与目标模型能力分布之间的兼容性，导致选出的数据可能不适合特定目标模型的训练需求。

Method: Data-DPO采用单步探测机制观察目标模型在不同样本上的局部训练反馈，将样本间的激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量级奖励模型以学习目标模型感知的数据偏好。最终选择阶段融合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边际多样性三个维度，构建更稳定有效的训练子集。

Result: 在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT两个基准上，Data-DPO在多种数据预算下持续优于现有数据选择基线方法，并稳定超越全量数据训练的性能表现。

Conclusion: Data-DPO通过引入目标模型感知的动态数据价值评估机制，有效解决了数据与模型兼容性不足的问题，为监督微调阶段的高效数据选择提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Data selection in supervised fine-tuning aims to select a small set of effective samples from large-scale candidate data, reducing training cost while preserving model performance. However, existing methods usually treat data value as a relatively static property, and pay limited attention to the compatibility between data and the capability distribution of the target model. To address this issue, we propose Data-DPO, a target model-oriented SFT data selection method. Data-DPO observes the local training feedback of the target model on different samples through one-step probing, transforms activation differences among samples into pairwise data preferences, and trains a lightweight reward model to learn target-model-aware data preferences. In the final selection stage, Data-DPO further combines target model preference, external quality scores, and marginal diversity to construct a more stable and effective training subset. Experimental results on Vision-Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that Data-DPO consistently outperforms existing data selection baselines under multiple data budgets and stably surpasses full data training performance.

</details>


### [52] [Hierarchical Data Selection via Manifold Coverage and Sparse Feature Coverage in LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16927)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MASS，一种用于监督微调数据选择的层次化覆盖方法，通过粗粒度语义分组和细粒度质量感知稀疏特征覆盖，在多个数据集上优于基线，且在小数据子集下达到或超越全量训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在原始嵌入空间直接度量多样性，几何指标混淆了主导语义方向、细粒度监督差异和局部噪声，导致选择效果不佳。

Method: 将数据选择建模为粗到细的层次化覆盖问题：先用稠密自编码器学习低维主流形坐标进行粗语义分组，再在每组内用 TopK 稀疏自编码器进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖。

Result: 在 Vision Flan 和 LLaVA-CoT 上，MASS 在多个预算下持续优于强基线，部分设置下仅用小部分数据即匹配或超越全量训练。

Conclusion: MASS 通过层次化覆盖有效解耦语义与噪声，实现高效高质量的数据选择，显著降低训练成本并提升模型性能。

Abstract: As supervised fine-tuning data continues to scale, selecting high-value subsets from large candidate pools is crucial for reducing training cost and improving model performance. Existing methods often measure diversity directly in the original embedding space, where geometric metrics entangle dominant semantic directions, fine-grained supervision differences, and local noise. We address this limitation by formulating data selection as a coarse-to-fine hierarchical coverage problem and propose MASS. MASS learns low-dimensional principal manifold coordinates with a dense autoencoder for coarse semantic grouping, and then performs quality-aware sparse feature coverage within each group using a TopK sparse autoencoder. Experiments on Vision Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that MASS consistently outperforms strong data selection baselines across multiple budgets, and in several settings matches or surpasses full data training with only a small subset of data.

</details>


### [53] [Benchmarking Classical and Transformer-Based Models for Document Sensitivity Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16928)
*Aleesha Zainab,Muhammad Ahmed Khalid,Faheem Ullah Khan,Asifullah Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文构建了首个受控标签泄露的敏感度分类基准 Strategic 16K，基于 WikiLeaks PlusD 外交电报，系统评估了六种模型架构，BERT 表现最佳，TF-IDF+逻辑回归在计算成本更低的情况下表现强劲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织文档敏感度自动分类至关重要但研究不足，现有 AI 方法依赖训练数据完整性，而标签泄露（文档正文残留的分类标记）导致模型利用表面捷径而非真实敏感信号，性能估计被夸大且不可靠。

Method: 构建 Strategic 16K 语料库（16,000 份外交电报），设计扩展泄露移除协议，识别并消除文档正文中三类残留分类标记；在清洗后的基准上评估六种模型架构，涵盖传统机器学习和 Transformer 模型。

Result: 在清洗基准上，BERT 表现最优（Accuracy 89.14%，F1 89.33%），ELECTRA 紧随其后（Accuracy 88.57%，F1 88.90%）；传统模型中 TF-IDF+逻辑回归以显著更低计算成本达到最强性能。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个基于 WikiLeaks PlusD、显式控制标签泄露且完全可复现的敏感度分类基准，为后续研究提供了可靠的评估平台。

Abstract: Automatic sensitivity classification of organizational documents is a critical yet underserved problem, where the consequences of misclassification range from regulatory violations to security breaches. While AI-based approaches offer a scalable alternative to manual review, their reliability depends fundamentally on the integrity of training data. A pervasive but underreported problem in this domain is label leakage: residual classification markers embedded within document bodies that allow models to exploit surface shortcuts rather than learning genuine content-based sensitivity signals, producing performance estimates that are inflated and unreliable. This paper addresses this problem by introducing Strategic 16K, a carefully constructed, leakage-controlled corpus of 16,000 diplomatic cables sourced from the WikiLeaks Public Library of US Diplomacy (PlusD), and presents a systematic benchmark evaluating six model architectures spanning classical machine learning and transformer-based approaches. We document an extended leakage removal protocol that identifies and eliminates three categories of residual classification markers embedded within document bodies. On the clean benchmark, BERT achieves the strongest performance (Accuracy = 89.14%, F1 = 89.33%), followed by ELECTRA (Accuracy = 88.57%, F1 = 88.90%). Among classical models, TF-IDF with Logistic Regression achieves the strongest performance at significantly lower computational cost. These results constitute the first fully reproducible sensitivity classification benchmark constructed under explicit leakage-controlled conditions from WikiLeaks PlusD.

</details>


### [54] [Mr.Dec: Daily-Scale Longitudinal Multimodal Modeling for 30-Day Readmission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16929)
*Minjun Kim,Jong Hak Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了Mr.Dec（多模态再入院风险预测解码器），通过Transformer解码器将每日电子病历(EHR)更新和间歇性胸部X光(CXR)发现建模为时间对齐的日级多模态事件序列，并引入疾病特定监督对比学习作为辅助正则化，在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上达到了最先进的30天再入院预测性能，同时能识别住院期间的'关键日'提供临床可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将复杂的纵向病史压缩为固定表示，丢失了反映患者生理状态动态演变的颗粒度日级临床信号。由于临床风险随住院期间证据积累而演变，捕捉这些动态轨迹对于准确预测30天再入院至关重要。

Method: Mr.Dec将每次入院建模为日级多模态事件的自然时间序列。利用Transformer解码器在时间对齐的流中整合每日EHR更新和间歇性CXR发现，反映实际临床工作流。采用疾病特定监督对比学习作为辅助正则化，在潜在空间中诱导诊断感知的结构以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集评估中，Mr.Dec通过保持临床序列完整性实现了最先进的预测性能。模型还能识别住院期间的'关键日'，为实时风险分层提供可操作且临床落地的解释。

Conclusion: Mr.Dec通过保留临床序列的时间完整性和多模态融合，显著提升了30天再入院预测性能。其识别关键日的能力为临床决策提供了实时、可解释的风险分层支持，代码已开源。

Abstract: Predicting 30-day hospital readmission is essential for assessing patient stability and optimizing healthcare resources. As clinical risk evolves with the accumulation of evidence during hospitalization, capturing these dynamic trajectories is essential. However, many existing approaches compress the complex longitudinal history into fixed representations, often losing the granular, day-level clinical signals that reflect a patient's evolving physiological state. To address this, we propose Mr.Dec (Multimodal Readmission-risk prediction Decoder), which models each admission as a natural chronological sequence of daily multimodal events. By leveraging a Transformer Decoder, Mr.Dec integrates daily Electronic Health Record(EHR) updates and intermittent Chest X-ray(CXR) findings in a time-aligned stream, reflecting the actual clinical workflow. To ensure robustness, we utilize Disease-Specific Supervised Contrastive Learning as an auxiliary regularization to induce a diagnosis-aware structure in the latent space. Evaluations on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets show that Mr.Dec achieves state-of-the-art performance by preserving the integrity of the clinical sequence. Furthermore, our model identifies "Critical Days" within an admission, providing actionable and clinically grounded interpretations for real-time risk stratification. Code is available at: https://github.com/yejix-ai/MR.DEC

</details>


### [55] [EMAN: Optimization-Driven Capacity Growth through Path Emergence in Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16930)
*Chenlei Fang,Jingchen Li,Hongzong LI,Qingyao Li,Yixuan Zhang,Huarui Wu,Haobin Shi,Chunjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Emergent Modular Atomic Network (EMAN)的优化驱动框架，解决现有多任务学习方法中结构变化受限于预定义结构或任务边界/冲突信号的问题。EMAN从单路径计算起步，通过潜在相对相位暴露反对称生长方向，在训练中监控多个决策信号，仅在持续优化证据出现时才实例化第二条独立路径，自适应分配共享与任务特定表示容量。在受控秩设置、PASCAL-Context和NYUv2上的实验验证了其有效性，以具有竞争力的计算成本获得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多任务学习方法（硬共享、多路径/专家、自适应共享、动态扩展）的容量变化通常受限于预定义结构或由任务边界和冲突信号触发，缺乏从单路径自发生长新独立路径的机制。核心问题是：网络能否从精确的单路径计算开始，仅在出现持久优化证据时才生长新的独立路径？

Method: 提出Emergent Modular Atomic Network (EMAN)，一种优化驱动框架：1) 通过潜在相对相位暴露反对称生长方向，而不实例化第二条路径；2) 训练过程中监控多个决策信号，将局部优化证据转化为结构决策；3) 仅在认证后才实例化两条等容量独立路径；4) 自适应分配共享与任务特定表示容量以适应不同任务需求。

Result: 在受控秩设置、PASCAL-Context和NYUv2数据集上的大量实验验证了EMAN的有效性，在具有竞争力的计算成本下实现了性能提升。

Conclusion: EMAN通过优化驱动的自发生长机制，解决了多任务学习中结构僵化的问题，实现了从单路径到双独立路径的证据驱动转变，自适应平衡共享与任务特定表示，为动态网络结构学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing multi-task learning methods rely on hard sharing, multiple paths or experts, adaptive sharing, and dynamic expansion. However, their capacity changes are usually constrained by predefined structures or triggered by task boundaries and conflict signals. This raises a fundamental question: can a network start from exact single-path computation and grow a new independent path only when persistent optimization evidence appears? We propose the Emergent Modular Atomic Network (EMAN), an optimization-driven framework for exposing an antisymmetric growth direction through latent relative phases without instantiating a second path, and for monitoring multiple decision signals during training to transform local optimization evidence into a structural decision. EMAN materializes two equal-capacity independent paths only after certification. EMAN adaptively allocates shared and task-specific representation capacity to accommodate varying task requirements. Extensive experiments on controlled rank settings, PASCAL-Context, and NYUv2 validate its effectiveness, achieving improved performance at a competitive computational cost.

</details>


### [56] [SW-ProxyCE: Zero-Query Adversarial Transfer from Public EEG Encoders to Private Downstream Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16931)
*Linhua Cong,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究EEG基础模型部署中的对抗迁移攻击，针对公开编码器与私有下游模型的场景，提出无需查询的任务感知攻击框架SW-ProxyCE，利用收缩白化类原型从少量标签参考集恢复任务决策几何，生成可迁移的对抗样本，实验表明该攻击有效威胁不可访问的下游模型，且优于任务无关的表示偏移攻击，揭示EEG基础模型的强迁移性不代表对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型编码器的公开发布虽促进下游开发，但引入未被探索的安全风险：公开表示可能使私有下游模型易受攻击。现有攻击多需查询受害模型或训练代理分类器，而实际场景中攻击者仅拥有公开编码器白盒访问权和少量任务匹配标签数据，无法接触受害模型参数、输出或梯度，因此需要一种免查询、任务感知的迁移攻击方法。

Method: 提出Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE)框架：利用少量标签参考集计算各类别的收缩白化原型，重构任务级决策边界几何；基于公开编码器的白盒梯度，直接优化对抗扰动使编码器输出向目标类原型靠近、远离源类原型，无需训练额外代理分类器，实现免查询的任务感知对抗样本生成。

Result: 在三个EEG任务、三个通用基础编码器及一个范式特定预训练编码器上评估，涵盖线性探针和全微调下游模型、跨被试和被试内场景。SW-ProxyCE生成的对抗样本能有效迁移攻击不可访问的下游模型，且在各种设置下持续优于任务无关的表示偏移攻击基线。

Conclusion: 公开EEG基础编码器配合少量标签参考数据即可构建强迁移攻击，揭示基础模型的强表示迁移性并不天然带来对抗鲁棒性，部署时需考虑此类安全威胁并开发相应防御机制。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for EEG decoding by learning reusable representations from large-scale heterogeneous neural recordings. However, the open release of EEG foundation encoders, while facilitating downstream developments, also introduces a previously unexplored security risk: publicly available representations may make private downstream models vulnerable.
