This paper investigates adversarial transfer attacks in EEG foundation model deployment in a public-encoder and private-downstream setting, where attackers have white-box access to a released encoder and a small task-matched labeled reference set, but no access or query to victim parameters, outputs, or gradients. We propose Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE), a query-free task-aware attack framework that recovers task-level decision geometry from a small labeled reference set through shrinkage-whitened class prototypes, enabling transferable adversarial generation without training an additional surrogate classifier. We evaluated SW-ProxyCE across three EEG tasks using three general-purpose foundation encoders and a paradigm-specific pre-trained encoder, covering both linear-probing and full-fine-tuning downstream models in cross-subject and within-subject scenarios. Results demonstrated that adversarial examples generated from the public encoder and limited labeled references can effectively transfer to inaccessible downstream models. SW-ProxyCE consistently outperformed task-agnostic representation-shift attacks, revealing that the strong transferability of EEG foundation models does not necessarily lead to adversarial robustness. Our code will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [57] [DOW-KE: Anchor-Free Multi-Layer Knowledge Editing via Direct End-to-End Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16932)
*Ran Chen,Junbo Zhang,Qianli Zhou,Xinyang Deng,Wen Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 DOW-KE，一种无锚点的知识编辑方法，通过在完整模型上反向传播编辑目标，联合优化所有编辑层的权重更新，解决了传统“先定位后编辑”流程中锚点目标与实际部署更新之间的闭包缺口，在大规模连续编辑实验中取得了最优的整体得分和邻域特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多层“定位-编辑”方法先优化中间层残差流激活（锚点），再逐层转化为权重更新，但各层更新来自局部求解，跨层传播导致的衰减和失真未被校正，导致锚点目标与实际部署编辑之间存在闭包缺口。

Method: DOW-KE 采用无锚点原则：优化目标必须与部署目标完全一致。通过在完整模型上反向传播最终编辑目标，联合优化所有编辑层的更新，使跨层传播和耦合进入每一步梯度；将保护性投影嵌入更新参数化内部（计算图内），使每一步梯度直接作用于部署后的更新，避免事后约束重新打开缺口。

Result: 在两个数据集和三个模型上的大规模连续编辑实验中，DOW-KE 在六个模型-数据集设置中的五个上获得了最高的整体 Score 和邻域 Specificity。

Conclusion: DOW-KE 通过端到端联合优化和内嵌保护机制，有效消除了锚点方法的闭包缺口，在保持知识完整性的同时实现了更精准、鲁棒的知识编辑，显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Multi-layer locate-then-edit methods for knowledge editing first optimize target residual-stream activations (anchors) at selected layers, then realize them layer by layer as weight updates. This pipeline optimizes an intermediate representation but deploys multi-layer weight updates whose joint effect through the true forward pass is never itself optimized: regardless of how anchors are set or propagated, each update comes from a local solve, so propagation-induced attenuation and distortion go uncorrected, leaving a closure gap between anchor targets and realized edits. We propose DOW-KE, an anchor-free method built on a single principle: what is optimized must be exactly what is deployed. DOW-KE backpropagates the final editing objective through the complete model, jointly optimizing the updates of all edited layers so cross-layer propagation and coupling enter every gradient step. The same principle dictates where preservation resides: embedding the preservation projection in the update parameterization, inside the computation graph, makes every gradient act on the deployed update; post-hoc constraints would reopen the gap, and the constrained search keeps edits clear of protected knowledge. In large-scale sequential editing on two datasets and three models, DOW-KE achieves the highest overall Score and neighborhood Specificity in five of six model-dataset settings among the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [58] [Study-Strategy Clusters from EdNet Logs Track Engagement, Not Mastery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16963)
*Qingchuan Lyu,Yingxin Li,Albert Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文测试了将ITS日志无监督聚类作为学习者类型预测学习效果的假设，发现行为聚类主要描述学习风格和参与度，而非知识增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证学习分析中常见假设：无监督聚类ITS日志得到的学习者类型能预测学习效果。

Method: 在EdNet-KT3数据集上，对5000名活跃学习者的学习策略特征（资源使用、复习、视频、刷题）进行聚类，轮廓系数选择k=5得到4个极端类别加一个大均值残差类，再对残差类细分得到8种稳定策略。将时间线按响应数量对半分，早期聚类预测后期参与度和正确率，对比知识追踪模型SAKT预测效果。

Result: 早期聚类能预测后期参与度（坚持性η²≈0.106，完成度η²≈0.021），但不能预测后期无辅助正确率（p_adj≈0.093）。仅基于活动量聚类与策略标签匹配度低（ARI=0.064）。SAKT模型仅比仅知板块难度基线略好（AUC提升+0.051），且掌握信号与行为风格几乎独立（ARI=0.007）。

Conclusion: 行为聚类在此描述的是学习风格和参与度，而非知识增益。

Abstract: Learning analytics often treats unsupervised clusters of intelligent tutoring system (ITS) logs as learner types that should predict learning. We test that assumption on EdNet-KT3. Clustering study-strategy features (resource use, revision, video, problem practice) for 5{,}000 active learners yields a silhouette-selected parent cut ($k=5$) with 4 contrast poles (reading-focused, video-heavy, revision-heavy, and problem-first) plus a large near-mean residual ($\sim$64.9\%). Reclustering that residual adds four finer styles, giving a bootstrap-stable hierarchy of 8 named strategies. We split each learner's timeline by respond count so clusters use only the early half and outcomes only the late half. Early clusters predict later engagement (continuing to practice and finishing late sessions, especially persistence, $η^{2}\approx 0.106$; completion $η^{2}\approx 0.021$) but not later unassisted accuracy (correctness on late first-attempts without help; $p_{\mathrm{adj}}\approx 0.093$). Volume rises with some styles, yet volume-only clustering barely matches strategy labels (ARI$=0.064$). A knowledge-tracing model (SAKT) on the seven TOEIC exam sections predicts next correctness only modestly better than a baseline that knows only how hard each section usually is (AUC lift $+0.051$; CI $[+0.045,+0.058]$), and that mastery signal is nearly independent of behavior styles (ARI$=0.007$). Behavioral clustering here describes study styles and engagement, not knowledge gains.

</details>


### [59] [RoBell-RVFL: A Robust Generalized Bell Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16965)
*A. Rahaman,A. Quadir,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoBell-RVFL 是一种针对类别不平衡和标签噪声的鲁棒轻量级广义钟形随机向量功能链接网络，通过双策略样本级加权机制保留少数类信息并自适应抑制多数类噪声样本，在保持闭式学习效率的同时显著优于现有 RVFL 变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集中多数类主导导致随机神经网络决策边界偏向多数类，忽略少数类样本；现有方法如 SMOTE 和类别加权损失仅关注类别比例，忽视类内分布，易受标签噪声和异常值影响。

Method: 提出 RoBell-RVFL，采用双策略样本级加权：少数类样本赋予单位权重严格保留信息；多数类样本在核诱导特征空间中通过概率加权广义钟形隶属度函数自适应调节影响力，显式融入局部类概率和类分布信息，抑制噪声、边界和异常样本，同时保持 RVFL 闭式学习效率。

Result: 在 UCI 和 KEEL 基准数据集上进行广泛评估，并在高达 40% 标签噪声下进行鲁棒性测试，RoBell-RVFL 持续且显著优于最新最先进的 RVFL 变体。

Conclusion: 自适应、质量感知的样本加权对于鲁棒 RVFL 学习至关重要，传统全局加权方案在噪声和不平衡环境中失效。

Abstract: The dominance of majority classes in real-world datasets poses a fundamental challenge to randomized neural networks, often biasing decision boundaries and overlooking critical minority samples. Existing remedies, such as synthetic minority over-sampling (SMOTE) and class-weighted loss functions, primarily address class proportions while neglecting intra-class distribution, making them vulnerable to label noise and outliers. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RoBell-RVFL}, a robust and lightweight \emph{quality-aware} generalized bell random vector functional link network that redefines how randomized models handle class imbalance and noisy data. RoBell-RVFL employs a dual-strategy, sample-level weighting mechanism that strictly preserves minority class information using unit weights, while adaptively regulating the influence of majority class samples through a probability-weighted generalized bell (gbell) membership function in a kernel-induced feature space. This design effectively suppresses noisy, boundary, and outlier samples within the majority class, enabling the network to learn from informative samples rather than merely abundant ones. By explicitly incorporating local class probability and class distribution information into the learning process, RoBell-RVFL achieves adaptive control over sample contributions without sacrificing the closed-form learning efficiency of RVFL networks. Extensive evaluations on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, along with robustness tests under up to 40\% label noise, demonstrate that RoBell-RVFL consistently and significantly outperforms recent state-of-the-art RVFL variants. The results indicate that adaptive, quality-aware sample weighting is essential for robust RVFL learning, rendering conventional global weighting schemes ineffective in noisy and imbalanced environments.

</details>


### [60] [MultiSigBERT: Beyond Survival Analysis through Multimodal and Sequential Modeling in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16972)
*Paul Minchella,Stéphane Chrétien,Guillaume Metzler,Loïc Verlingue,Rémi Vaucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MultiSigBERT框架，利用签名变换对多模态EHR时序数据进行高阶交互建模，结合LASSO-Cox实现肿瘤生存预测，在真实队列上取得C-index 0.743。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生存模型多局限于单一模态或忽视患者轨迹的时序动态，而电子健康记录（EHR）包含叙事报告、数值测量和结构化变量等互补多模态信息，亟需统一框架联合建模多模态时序动态与患者几何结构以提升预测性能。

Method: 1) 叙事医疗报告经BERT提取上下文词嵌入并平均得到句向量；2) 各模态经PCA压缩后与结构化协变量拼接形成联合时序轨迹；3) 引入粗路径理论中的签名变换，无监督编码跨模态高阶时序交互；4) 将签名特征作为高维特征输入LASSO正则化Cox模型，输出个体化风险评分。

Result: 在莱昂·贝纳尔中心真实肿瘤队列（>2500例患者，>12万份报告）上，独立测试集一致性指数（C-index）达0.743（标准差0.029），验证了联合建模多模态时序动态与患者几何结构对生存预测的显著增益。

Conclusion: MultiSigBERT通过签名变换高效捕捉异构EHR数据的跨模态高阶时序交互，结合稀疏Cox模型实现了高精度生存预测，为临床决策支持提供了可解释、可扩展的多模态时序建模新范式。

Abstract: Machine learning has become an essential component of modern healthcare, where the integration of heterogeneous data sources offers unprecedented opportunities to improve clinical decision-making. Electronic Health Records (EHR) contain complementary information -- including narrative clinical reports, numerical measurements, and structured variables -- yet most survival models remain limited to a single modality or fail to exploit the temporal nature of patient trajectories. We propose MultiSigBERT, a unified framework for multimodal sequential survival modeling in oncology based on path signature representations. Here, narrative medical reports (free-text) are converted into sentence embeddings by extracting and averaging contextual word embeddings. These representations are then compressed via modality-specific PCA and concatenated with structured covariates to form joint temporal trajectories which are then encoded using the Signature transform, a tool from Rough Paths theory that efficiently captures higher-order temporal interactions across modalities without supervision needed. The computed Signature features are finally incorporated as high dimensional features into a LASSO-regularized Cox model to estimate individualized risk scores. The performance of our novel MultiSigBERT pipeline is illustrated on the analysis of a real-world oncology cohort from the Léon Bérard Center, comprising over 120,000 medical reports and structured records from more than 2,500 patients. The model achieves a concordance index of 0.743 (sd 0.029) on an independent test set, demonstrating the benefit of jointly modeling multimodal temporal dynamics together with patient-level geometric structure for survival prediction.

</details>


### [61] [Position: Fairness Failure in Generative Models is an Evaluation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16974)
*Mariia Vladimirova,Jean-Yves Franceschi,Thibaut Issenhuth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一份立场论文，指出生成模型的公平性失败根源在于评估问题：公平性发现缺乏可比性和可操作性。作者诊断了当前实践中的经验性和概念性失效模式，提出“公平性卡片”作为最小报制品，使评估选择（提示族、反事实协议、指标、拒答处理）显式化，以实现可复现性、可比性和问责制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成模型取得突破性进展，但其公平性问题（强化社会不平等、伤害边缘群体）长期未被充分解决。核心原因在于公平性评估缺乏标准化：不同论文的发现难以比较，也难以指导部署决策。当前临时性的偏见检查存在反复出现的经验性和概念性失效模式。

Method: 作者提出“公平性卡片”作为标准化报告工件，包含四个核心维度：1) 提示族，2) 反事实协议，3) 评估指标，4) 拒答处理策略。通过使这些评估选择显式化，实现跨研究的可复现性、可比性和问责制。同时诊断当前实践中的常见失效模式，并给出评估标准范式转变的额外建议。

Result: 提出了Fairness Cards框架，为生成模型公平性评估提供最小化、标准化的报告格式。该框架已发布项目页面（https://mariiavladimirova.github.io/fairness-cards），旨在推动从临时性偏见检查向标准化、生成模型专用评估的范式转变。

Conclusion: 生成模型公平性评估需从临时性检查转向标准化、可比较的评估范式。Fairness Cards作为最小报告工件，通过显式化评估选择实现可复现性、可比性和问责制。作者呼吁建立统一的评估标准，以便公平性发现能真正指导部署决策，减少对边缘群体的伤害。}

Abstract: Despite groundbreaking advancements in generative models during the last decade, concerns about their lack of fairness, reinforcing societal inequalities and harming marginalized groups, remain under-addressed and difficult to act upon. This position paper argues that fairness failures in generative models, albeit driven by multiple factors, are ultimately stemming from an evaluation problem: fairness findings are rarely comparable across papers or actionable for deployment decisions. This paper diagnoses recurring empirical and conceptual failure modes in current practice and motivates a shift from ad-hoc bias checks to standardized, generative-specific evaluation. We propose Fairness Cards as a minimal reporting artifact that makes evaluation choices explicit (prompt families, counterfactual protocols, metrics, and refusal handling) enabling reproducibility, comparability, and accountability. We conclude with additional recommendations towards a paradigm shift in evaluation standards. Our project page can be found at https://mariiavladimirova.github.io/fairness-cards .

</details>


### [62] [Agents unlock new capabilities through Switching LoRA Adapters as a Tool (SLAaaT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17034)
*Kenneth Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种让智能体在推理过程中动态切换专用LoRA适配器的方法，解决后训练导致的灾难性遗忘问题，在合成编码任务上实现自主切换并显著降低能力损耗，优于子智能体生成策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练虽能提升专业任务性能，但常导致其他领域的灾难性遗忘，这在需要组合多种能力的长智能体轨迹中尤为突出。现有方法难以在同一推理过程中灵活结合多种专业能力。

Method: 赋予智能体在推理轨迹中途切换专用LoRA适配器的工具，使其能根据任务需求动态选择合适的适配器。在两个逻辑简单但需要专业化的合成编码任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 模型能解决先前无法解决的问题，能自主切换适配器并发现优于人类启发式基线的新策略，与仅使用单一专用适配器的智能体相比，能力损耗降低高达18倍。该方法在任务能力和Token使用量上均显著优于生成子智能体的策略。

Conclusion: 动态切换LoRA适配器有效缓解灾难性遗忘，使智能体在长轨迹中灵活组合多种专业能力，实现自主策略发现并大幅降低计算开销，为多能力智能体系统提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training can unlock new capabilities and improve performance on specialized tasks, but sometimes at the cost of catastrophic forgetting in other domains. This poses a problem in long agent trajectories that compose different capabilities. We reject this tradeoff by giving an agent a tool to switch between specialized LoRA adapters mid-trace. To test its effectiveness, we compose two synthetic coding tasks that are logically simple but require specialization. We find that this allows the model to solve problems it previously could not, that the model is able to switch autonomously (and find a new strategy that beats our human heuristic baseline on one task), and that this incurs an up to an 18x reduction in capability tax compared to an agent using only one specialized adapter. Our approach also substantially outperforms spawning subagents in both task capabilities and token usage.

</details>


### [63] [J-Miner: Recovering Executable Decision Knowledge from Language-Model Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17063)
*Yunfan Gao,Xinyi Huang,Tao Sheng,Haorui Song,Yun Xiong,Haofen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出J-Miner方法，从微调后的大语言模型分类器中挖掘内部决策知识，将其显式化为可执行的决策规则，实现了高保真度的知识蒸馏和迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型微调后虽能成为优秀的分类器，但其决策知识隐含在模型内部，仅输出最终标签，难以被检验、验证和复用。研究旨在将这些隐性知识显式化为可执行表示。

Method: J-Miner通过聚合跨层、跨token位置的词汇对齐内部信号来挖掘文本级命名概念，并利用分类器自身的预测在这些概念上学习可执行决策规则，将局部内部读出蒸馏为显式的分类器级知识表示。

Result: 在多个分类任务上，J-Miner规则复现了高达98.3%的源分类器决策，行为保真度比基于输入词学习的同等紧凑规则高6.0-29.5个百分点。命名概念反映了与任务决策相关的内部语义证据，规则将这些分布式信号整合为可检查的决策结构。所得决策知识可迁移到轻量级独立学生模型：仅用源分类器约1/24的参数量，从原始文本重构并执行该表示，保留了源分类器平均任务准确率的99.8%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，任务特定的决策知识可以忠实地用显式、可执行的形式表示，并能在学习它的分类器之外被复用。

Abstract: Large language models can be fine-tuned into specialized classifiers that perform well across diverse text tasks and make complex judgments, but they typically expose only final labels, leaving the decision knowledge acquired through fine-tuning implicit within the model. We study how to mine this internal decision knowledge from a fine-tuned classifier and encode it in an executable representation that can be inspected, validated, and reused beyond the source classifier. We introduce J-Miner, which mines text-level named concepts by aggregating vocabulary-aligned internal signals across layers and token positions, and uses the classifier's own predictions to learn executable decision rules over them. This process distills local internal readouts into an explicit classifier-level knowledge representation. Across multiple classification tasks, J-Miner rules reproduce up to 98.3\% of source-classifier decisions and achieve 6.0--29.5 percentage points higher behavioral fidelity than equally compact rules learned from input words. Further analysis shows that the named concepts reflect internal semantic evidence associated with task decisions, while the learned rules consolidate these distributed signals into inspectable decision structures. The resulting decision knowledge also transfers to lightweight standalone students: using about 1/24 as many parameters as the source classifiers, they reconstruct and execute the representation from raw text while retaining 99.8\% of the source classifiers' mean task accuracy. These findings show that task-specific decision knowledge can be faithfully represented in an explicit, executable form and reused beyond the classifier in which it was learned.

</details>


### [64] [Certified but Private: Scalable Zero-Knowledge Proofs for Neural Network Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17070)
*Youwei Zhong,Ben Merbaum,Timos Antonopoulos,Ning Luo,Charalampos Papamanthou,Katerina Sotiraki,Ruzica Piskac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PANDA 是一个可扩展系统，利用零知识证明在不泄露模型参数的情况下证明神经网络的鲁棒性和公平性，基于 CROWN 框架，通过新的线性松弛界证明算法实现多项式级扩展，支持百万级参数网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习模型广泛部署，对安全关键和法律合规场景中模型鲁棒性和公平性的形式化保证需求增加，但模型参数通常是商业机密，无法向审计员或用户公开。

Method: 基于 CROWN 鲁棒性认证框架，设计新算法证明非线性激活层的线性松弛界，生成简单轻量的零知识证明，实现证明者开销多项式级扩展。

Result: 能为超过 290 万参数的神经网络在 5 分钟内生成局部鲁棒性证明，验证仅需 10 秒；比先前基于 ZKP 的系统（指数时间算法）扩展大 4 个数量级，证明者开销显著降低。

Conclusion: PANDA 通过零知识证明实现了大规模神经网络鲁棒性和公平性的可验证审计，在不泄露模型参数前提下提供了形式化保证，解决了商业机密与审计透明度的矛盾。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of machine learning models, formal guarantees of the robustness and fairness of these models have become increasingly important in safety-critical and legal-compliance settings. However, model parameters are often commercial secrets that cannot be disclosed to auditors or end users. To this end, we present PANDA, a scalable system that uses zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) to prove the robustness and fairness properties of a model without revealing its private parameters. PANDA is built on top of CROWN, an efficient robustness certification framework that is used in many state-of-the-art formal verification tools for neural networks. The core contribution of PANDA is a novel algorithm for proving linear relaxation bounds for non-linear activation layers, yielding simple, lightweight proofs. Remarkably, our system can generate proofs of local robustness for neural networks with more than 2.9M parameters in 5 minutes, and can verify them in 10 seconds. Prior ZKP-based robustness system rely on exponential-time algorithms that cannot scale to nontrivial networks. In contrast, PANDA scales polynomially in the number of neurons in a network, allowing us to support neural networks 4 orders of magnitude larger than previous approaches with significantly reduced prover overhead.

</details>


### [65] [Dynamic Regime-Aware Conformal Calibration for Reliable Economic Forecast Intervals under Multiple Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17079)
*Bogdan Oancea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dynamic Regime-Aware Conformal Prediction (DRACP)，解决经济预测中协变量偏移、概念漂移、局部异质性和潜在机制导致的可交换性假设失效问题。DRACP 将密度比、局部核、概率机制感知加权与自调谐在线显著性控制器融合在统一的加权共形校准框架中。理论上给出有限样本效度、覆盖率差距界和在线控制器的遗憾保证。在 48 个真实预测序列（欧元区/欧盟 27 国 HICP 通胀、美国宏观与能源指标、日度金融序列）上评测：强自适应在线共形预测区间最窄（约窄 20%）且区间得分最优，但 DRACP 校准最可靠，覆盖率最接近名义 0.90 (0.890)，从未低于 0.80，所有预测步长均保持最佳覆盖，且在 2021-2023 通胀激增期表现最强。强自适应方法在 20 个序列上欠覆盖，DRACP 仅 10 个。消融实验显示在线控制器与条件尺度归一化贡献最大，加权组件贡献较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经济预测中常出现协变量偏移、概念漂移、局部异质性和潜在机制变化，导致标准共形预测所需的可交换性假设失效，现有在线共形方法在分布偏移下覆盖率不稳定，亟需一种能同时应对多种分布偏移并提供可靠有限样本覆盖保证的方法。

Method: 提出 DRACP 框架：(1) 密度比加权处理协变量偏移；(2) 局部核加权捕捉局部异质性；(3) 概率机制感知加权建模潜在机制切换；(4) 自调谐在线显著性控制器动态调整误覆盖率。四者融合于统一的加权共形校准框架中，并提供三项理论保证：Oracle 重要性权重下的有限样本效度、估计权重的覆盖率差距界（基于有效样本量）、在线控制器的确定性/遗憾保证。

Result: 在 48 个真实预测序列上与六种基线（含 FACI、强自适应在线共形、共形 PID）对比：强自适应方法区间最窄（~20% 更窄）且区间得分最优，但 DRACP 校准最可靠——覆盖率最接近名义 0.90 (0.890)，无序列跌破 0.80，所有预测步长覆盖率均最佳，2021-2023 通胀激增期表现最强。强自适应方法在 20/48 序列欠覆盖，DRACP 仅 10/48。消融实验显示在线控制器与条件尺度归一化贡献主要性能增益，加权组件贡献较小。

Conclusion: DRACP 在校准可靠性与区间效率间提供原则性折中，倾向于在预测区间必须满足覆盖率标准时保证可靠覆盖。理论与实证均表明其对多种分布偏移具有鲁棒性，适合对覆盖率要求严格的经济预测场景。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free prediction intervals but relies on exchangeability, an assumption often violated in economic forecasting because of covariate shift, concept drift, local heterogeneity and latent regimes. We propose Dynamic Regime-Aware Conformal Prediction (DRACP), which combines density-ratio, localized kernel and probabilistic regime-aware weighting with a self-tuning online significance controller in a unified weighted conformal calibration framework. We distinguish three theoretical results: finite-sample validity under oracle importance weights, a coverage-gap bound for estimated weights with rates in effective sample size, and deterministic or regret guarantees for the online controller. We evaluate DRACP against six baselines on 48 real forecasting series covering euro-area and EU-27 HICP inflation, US macroeconomic and energy indicators, and daily financial series. Recent online methods (FACI, strongly-adaptive online conformal prediction and conformal PID) were verified against the authors' implementations. DRACP is not the most efficient method: strongly-adaptive online conformal prediction achieves the best interval score and intervals about 20% narrower. Instead, DRACP provides the most reliable calibration, achieving coverage closest to the nominal 0.90 (0.890), never falling below 0.80 on any series, maintaining the best coverage at all forecast horizons, and performing best during the 2021-2023 inflation surge. The strongly-adaptive method undercovers on 20 of 48 series versus 10 for DRACP. DRACP therefore offers a principled trade-off between calibration and efficiency, favoring reliable coverage when prediction intervals must satisfy coverage standards. An ablation study shows that the online controller and conditional-scale normalization provide most of the performance gain, whereas the weighting components make a smaller contribution.

</details>


### [66] [Backward through Time, Algebraically](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17087)
*Konstantinos Kogkalidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种可微分的线性时序逻辑评估引擎，支持多种语义代数，并作为PyTorch库telos发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性时序逻辑基于布尔值，难以应用于软值系统（如神经网络策略、自适应控制器等），需要可微分的语义来作为训练信号，但现有实现多为浅嵌入，强制预先选择单一语义代数，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 设计了一种代数通用的评估引擎，支持前向和反向传播，提供可执行的代数规范，实现并审计多种可微分语义代数，分析其前向和反向行为。

Result: 实现了多种可微分语义代数，发现每种代数都在某个方向上存在权衡（即“选择哪个方向让人失望，以及如何失望”），并将引擎作为PyTorch库telos开源。

Conclusion: 提供了一种灵活、可微分的线性时序逻辑评估框架，解决了软值系统中逻辑规范作为训练信号的问题，并开源了telos库供社区使用。

Abstract: Linear temporal logic is a modal extension of propositional logic that allows one to state how a system should behave over time. Its canonical domain is the booleans, but discretely-valued judgements are of little use in steering softly-valued systems (neural policies, adaptive controllers, sequence models, etc). In such cases, the goal formula's (dis)satisfaction becomes a training signal, and differentiability becomes a prime concern. Candidate differentiable semantics abound, but navigating them is tricky. Implementations, where available, are shallow embeddings, demanding an upfront commitment to a single semantic algebra and its (usually implicit) conduct. The paper casts the reader as a functional programmer asked to come to terms with this predicament, and refusing. Out of that refusal comes an evaluation engine that is algebra-generic and amenable to differentiation, together with an executable specification of the algebras it can accept. Various algebras are implemented and audited for their behavior, both forward and backward. Each algebra turns out to be a choice of which direction to disappoint, and how. Everything described (and more) is part of the PyTorch library telos, to be found at https://github.com/konstantinosKokos/telos.

</details>


### [67] [Deep Learning for Cross-Border Electricity Price Forecasting: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17091)
*Hadeer Elashhab,Sai Srijan Papineni,Marvin Dorn,Veit Hagenmeyer,Benjamin Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对电力价格预测（EPF）领域缺乏标准化基准数据集的问题，利用公开数据构建可复现评估框架，跨多个电力市场对比六种深度学习模型（含状态空间、MLP、RNN、Transformer架构），重点考察零样本、单样本、少样本等低数据场景下的泛化能力。实验聚焦2024年德国-卢森堡（DE-LU）竞价区，采用标准化日历、历史价格及市场衍生特征。结果显示N-HiTS与NBEATSx在小样本条件下表现优异，Transformer类模型虽可达相当精度但需更多调优；特征选择与超参数调整显著影响性能，顶尖模型间差异往往微小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EPF研究多依赖不同数据集和指标孤立评估，缺乏统一基准导致难以一致比较最新方法进展；且多数深度学习工作局限于单一市场、窄特征集或固定训练制度，亟需建立可复现的跨市场标准化评估框架，系统考察模型在数据稀缺场景下的泛化能力。

Method: 使用公开电力市场数据构建标准化数据集（含日历、历史价格、市场衍生特征），聚焦2024年DE-LU竞价区作为测试集。选取六种代表性深度学习模型涵盖状态空间、MLP、RNN、Transformer四大架构类别。设计零样本、单样本、少样本三种低数据目标市场模拟场景，统一训练/验证/测试流程与评估指标，进行跨市场泛化能力的对比实验，并分析特征选择与超参数调优的影响。

Result: N-HiTS与NBEATSx在零/单/少样本等有限数据场景下表现最具竞争力；Transformer基模型虽能达到相当预测精度，但通常需要更多领域适应与超参数调优；精心的特征选择与超参数调整能显著提升模型性能；排名靠前的几个最强模型之间的性能差异往往很小，无统计学显著优势。

Conclusion: 本工作为EPF建立了首个基于公开数据的跨市场标准化深度学习基准，证实了N-HiTS/NBEATSx等基于MLP/残差架构的模型在小样本条件下的鲁棒优势，揭示了Transformer类模型对数据量与调优的敏感性。研究强调特征工程与超参数优化的重要性，并指出当前顶尖模型性能已趋近，未来进步可能更多来自数据质量、外生变量融合及不确定性量化等方向，而非单纯架构创新。

Abstract: While publicly available electricity market data presents a valuable resource for forecasting research, the field lacks established benchmark datasets for standardized comparison. As a result, many studies have relied on different datasets and metrics to evaluate methods in isolated settings, making it difficult to assess progress and compare state-of-the-art approaches consistently. In this work, we use public data to evaluate deep learning models for electricity price forecasting (EPF) across multiple market settings. Our goal is to establish a reproducible framework that enables a consistent evaluation of forecasting models. Although deep learning has been explored for day-ahead EPF, many prior studies are limited to single-market settings, narrow feature sets, or fixed training regimes. This work presents a comparative evaluation of six deep learning models--covering state-space, MLP, RNN, and Transformer-based architectures--emphasizing generalization across markets. We simulate low-data target-market conditions using zero-shot, one-shot, and few-shot learning. Our test set focuses on the Germany-Luxembourg (DE-LU) bidding zone in 2024 using a standardized dataset with calendar, historical price, and market-derived features. Our findings suggest that N-HiTS and NBEATSx perform competitively in limited-data scenarios, while transformer-based models can reach comparable accuracy but tend to require more adaptation and tuning. Model performance also benefits from careful feature selection and hyperparameter tuning, and we note that the differences between the strongest models are often small.

</details>


### [68] [From Abductive Explanations to Global Logical Rules for Node Classification in SGCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17103)
*Bryan Lima Cavalcante,Thiago Alves Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于逻辑的框架，用于简单图卷积（SGC）网络的节点分类，利用最小归纳解释作为中间表示提取全局逻辑规则，实验表明生成的规则紧凑且保持高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于逻辑的方法（如LogicXGNN）从解释性子图衍生全局规则，但这些子图可能包含冗余的节点特定结构信息，限制了规则的泛化性。

Method: 对每个节点计算保持预测类别所需的最小节点-特征对集合（最小归纳解释），然后用这些解释训练决策树以提取全局逻辑规则。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，该框架生成的全局规则紧凑，同时保持对原始SGC模型的高保真度。

Conclusion: 该框架通过最小归纳解释有效提取紧凑且高保真的全局逻辑规则，解决了现有方法中冗余信息限制规则泛化性的问题。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable performance in node classification tasks, motivating growing interest in methods capable of explaining their predictions. Recent logic-based approaches, such as LogicXGNN, derive global logical rules for Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) from collections of explanatory subgraphs. While informative, these subgraphs may contain redundant structural information that is specific to individual nodes, potentially limiting the generality of the extracted rules. In this work, we propose a logic-based framework for node classification in Simple Graph Convolution (SGC) networks that uses minimal abductive explanations as an intermediate representation for rule extraction. For each node, we compute a minimal set of node-feature pairs sufficient to preserve the predicted class. These explanations are then used to train decision trees from which global logical rules are extracted. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that the proposed framework produces compact global rules while maintaining high fidelity to the original SGC model.

</details>


### [69] [Causal Discovery in Equal Variance Linear Gaussian DAGs via SURE-Tuned Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17132)
*Sambit Mishra,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SURE-Ridge是一种非迭代闭式估计器，用于等方差线性高斯SEM，通过SURE自适应选择正则化参数和自适应阈值提取DAG，在小样本和计算受限场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续优化方法（如NOTEARS、DAGMA）依赖迭代梯度下降和逐问题超参数调优，不适合样本受限（样本数可比或少于节点数）和计算受限场景。

Method: 提出SURE-Ridge：对每个节点并行进行岭回归，利用Stein无偏风险估计（SURE）自适应选择正则化参数，得到软邻接矩阵，再通过自适应阈值处理提取DAG。

Result: 在小样本 regime 下实现最低结构汉明距离，在所有测试样本量下运行时间最短，优于NOTEARS、DAGMA和GBNSL基线。

Conclusion: SURE-Ridge为因果发现提供了高效、无需迭代的解决方案，特别适合样本有限和计算资源受限的实际应用。

Abstract: Recovering the directed acyclic graph (DAG) of a structural equation model (SEM) from observational data is a central problem in causal discovery. The iterative gradient descent and per-problem hyperparameter tuning of continuous-optimization methods are poorly suited to two practically important regimes: the sample-limited regime, where the number of samples is comparable to or smaller than the number of nodes in the DAG, and the compute-limited regime. This work proposes SURE-Ridge, a non-iterative, closed-form estimator for equal variance linear Gaussian SEM. The method performs parallel node-wise regressions with regularization parameters chosen adaptively by Stein's unbiased risk estimate (SURE), and applies an adaptive thresholding procedure to extract a DAG from the resulting soft adjacency matrix. Numerical results show that SURE-Ridge achieves the lowest structural Hamming distance in the small-sample regime and the lowest run time across all sample sizes tested, compared with NOTEARS, DAGMA, and GBNSL baselines.

</details>


### [70] [Iterative tensor network transformations for element-wise evaluation of elementary and filtering functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17135)
*Xiao Wang,Tomohiro Hashizume,Pia Siegl,Dieter Jaksch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代张量网络变换（ITNT），一种在压缩域中对张量火车（TT）编码数据进行逐元素非线性函数计算的通用框架，实现了对指数级大规模数据集的高效处理，并在三维反应流场计算和Max-SAT优化问题（高达2^70构型）上展示了强大能力，确立了张量网络方法在通用数据科学和大规模优化中的基础地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络虽擅长压缩大规模数据，但受限于难以执行非线性操作，在通用数据处理中的应用受限。迫切需要一种能在压缩域直接进行非线性逐元素计算的通用算法框架，以释放张量网络在数据科学和优化中的潜力。

Method: 提出迭代张量网络变换（ITNT）框架，完全在张量火车（TT）压缩域中运行，通过迭代方式实现初等函数和非线性滤波函数的逐元素计算，同时保持受控的计算成本，避免了显式解压缩带来的指数级开销。

Result: 在两个关键领域验证了有效性：(I) 对三维反应流场进行高度非线性的初等函数和滤波函数计算，实现高保真反应速率计算和区域筛选；(II) 求解复杂优化问题中的极值，成功处理高达2^70构型空间的Max-SAT实例。

Conclusion: ITNT为张量网络方法提供了通用非线性计算能力，使其成为通用数据科学和大规模优化的基础工具，突破了张量网络仅限于线性操作的局限，为指数级大规模数据的压缩域处理开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Tensor networks are powerful formats for compressing large-scale data. However, their application to general data processing has been limited by the difficulty of performing nonlinear operations. Here, we introduce iterative tensor network transformations (ITNTs), a general algorithmic framework for the element-wise evaluation of elementary and nonlinear filtering functions on data encoded as tensor trains (TTs), a class of tensor networks. Our approach operates entirely in the compressed domain, enabling efficient computation on exponentially large datasets while maintaining a controlled computational cost. We demonstrate its power in two key areas: (I) evaluating highly nonlinear elementary and filtering functions on a 3D reactive flow field, enabling high-fidelity reaction rate computation and region filtering, and (II) finding extrema in complex optimization problems, such as solving Max-SAT instances on spaces up to $2^{70}$ configurations. These results establish ITNT as a foundational tool that provides tensor network methods with the capability for general-purpose data science and large-scale optimization.

</details>


### [71] [OraclePhys: A Systematic Framework for LLM Fine-Tuning on Structural Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17162)
*Mingyu Li,Guorui Song,Jing Lin,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OraclePhys 是一个系统性微调框架，包含基准、数据集和受控实验，发现标签答案形式（而非位数）因果决定微调所学内容：排序目标安装分布外前向模型，标量目标仅部分有效，布尔目标无可检测效果；书面或分数过滤答案能装配能力，而优势加权分数（GRPO）虽提升奖励却使模型在保留物理任务上统计等同于初始模型，仅适合路由。训练的 8B 模型达到任务数据精度前沿，超越前沿 LLM 的零样本和 32-shot，达到专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型微调后的内化内容通常事后诊断，缺乏因果证据。需要系统框架将微调目标作为实验变量，研究不同答案形式、验证器角色对模型学习物理计算能力的因果影响。

Method: 构建 OraclePhys 三件套：1) OraclePhys-Bench，精确分级的结构力学基准，有限元 oracle 为每个答案和反事实编辑打分，无需人工标注或 LLM 评判；2) OraclePhys-30K，七种答案形式在字节级相同结构描述上的监督数据集；3) 跨七种答案形式和三种验证器角色的受控训练实验。

Result: 两大发现：1) 标签答案形式因果决定微调所教内容：排序目标安装分布外前向模型（基座模型处于猜测先验），标量目标最多安装部分前向模型，布尔目标无可检测安装；向量-标量鸿沉跨第二物理域、第二模型家族、改述评估面均存续。2) 书面或分数过滤答案安装该能力，而优势加权分数（GRPO）虽提升奖励，却使模型在保留物理任务上统计等同于初始模型，仅在测试配方和预算下足够用于路由。训练的 8B 模型达到任务数据精度前沿，零样本和 32-shot 均超越前沿 LLM，达到专家水平。

Conclusion: 标签中写明的目标计算内容正是微调所教内容；你在什么上训练，就路由什么。

Abstract: What a language model internalizes from fine-tuning is usually diagnosed after the fact. We make it an experimental variable. OraclePhys is a systematic fine-tuning framework with three components: OraclePhys-Bench, an exactly-graded structural-mechanics benchmark whose finite-element oracle scores every answer and counterfactual edit -- no human labels, no LLM judging; OraclePhys-30K, a supervision dataset of seven answer forms over byte-identical structure descriptions; and a controlled training study across the seven forms and three verifier roles. The study yields two findings. First, the label's answer form -- not its bit count -- causally determines what fine-tuning teaches: a ranking objective installs an out-of-distribution forward model where the untrained base sits at the guessing prior, a scalar objective at best a partial one, a boolean nothing detectable; the vector-scalar gulf survives a second physics domain, a second model family, and a paraphrased evaluation surface. Second, written or score-filtered answers install this capability, while advantage-weighted scores (GRPO) raise reward yet leave the model statistically equivalent to its start on held-out physics -- within the recipes and budgets tested -- sufficing only for routing. The trained 8B -- the first LLM on spatial structural response -- reaches the task's data-precision frontier: above a frontier LLM at zero- and 32-shot, at a specialist's level. What the label spells out about the target computation is what fine-tuning teaches; what you train on is what you route.

</details>


### [72] [Q-Learning With World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17163)
*Perry Dong,Yueru Jia,Chelsea Finn,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QWM框架，将世界模型与Q-learning结合，通过测试时搜索想象轨迹来选择高价值动作，在保持策略和价值函数仅在真实数据上训练的同时，避免了复合模型偏差，显著提升了样本效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的RL方法通常直接在想象轨迹上优化策略或价值函数，容易产生复合偏差，且难以扩展到大规模、高维问题（如真实机器人）。本文旨在探索是否可以将世界模型直接应用于标准Q-learning之上，在保持在线真实环境训练的前提下提升性能。

Method: 提出QWM框架：利用世界模型进行测试时搜索，在想象轨迹上评估动作价值，结合Q-learning选择高价值动作；策略和价值函数仅在真实转移数据上训练，避免模型偏差积累。

Result: 在Robomimic和LIBERO两个具有挑战性的操作基准上，QWM在样本效率和最终性能上均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: QWM证明了将世界模型用于测试时搜索而非训练时想象优化，能够有效结合模型预测的样本效率优势与无模型RL的稳健性，为真实世界机器人任务提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Off-policy reinforcement learning (RL) has become increasingly sample-efficient, enabling applications such as RL fine-tuning of Vision-Language-Action models into reliable, high-performing policies. World models offer a further lever for sample efficiency, as they predict state changes rather than actions alone, but their success has largely been confined to supervised policy learning. Prior model-based RL methods often optimize the policy or value function directly on imagined rollouts, which is prone to compounding bias and struggles to scale to large, high-dimensional problems such as real-world robotics, a problem that worsens with task horizon and visual complexity. In this work, we instead ask whether we can leverage world models directly on top of standard Q-learning to improve performance, while remaining trained and grounded in the real, online setting. We propose QWM, a framework that leverages world models to perform test-time search over imagined trajectories on top of Q-learning to select high-value actions during both online rollouts and evaluation. Since the policy and value function are trained only on real transitions, QWM avoids compounding model bias while still gaining the sample-efficiency benefits of predictive search. On challenging manipulation benchmarks Robomimic and LIBERO, QWM significantly outperforms strong prior state-of-the-art methods on both sample efficiency and performance.

</details>


### [73] [SCENARIODIFF: A Scenario-level Guidance Framework for Multimodal Time Series Forecasting--Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17164)
*Tuan-Binh Tran,Dat Nguyen Cong,Duc-Trong Le,Thanh Trung Huynh,Tung Kieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCENARIODIFF 是一个分层情境推理框架，用于在噪声和弱对齐文档下的多模态时间序列预测。它通过三个代理提取历史证据、生成定性场景描述和稀疏锚点，结合多模态扩散 Transformer 和锚点混合采样实现可解释且可控的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态预测方法要么让大语言模型直接预测数值，要么隐式融合文本和时间序列，导致上下文影响难以解释和控制。文本上下文（新闻、报告、日志）能为时间序列预测提供有价值信号，特别是当未来动态由历史数值中不可见的外部事件驱动时。

Method: SCENARIODIFF 将上下文信息组织为三个层级：历史上下文代理从原始文档中提取逐步证据；场景代理为预测时段生成定性场景描述；锚点引导代理为事件相关的未来区域生成稀疏锚点。这些结构化信号作为条件输入到多模态扩散 Transformer 中，并通过锚点混合采样在无需重新训练的情况下局部细化生成轨迹。

Result: 在 Time-MMD 基准上的实验表明，SCENARIODIFF 在事件驱动领域表现尤为出色，验证了显式分层场景引导对多模态时间序列预测的价值。

Conclusion: 研究证明了显式分层场景引导能有效提升多模态时间序列预测的性能和可解释性，为处理噪声和弱对齐文档下的事件驱动预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Textual context such as news, reports, and logs can provide valuable signals for time series forecasting, especially when future dynamics are driven by external events that are not yet visible in historical values. Existing multimodal forecasting methods often either ask large language models (LLMs) to predict numerical values directly or fuse text and time series implicitly, making contextual influence difficult to interpret and control. We propose SCENARIODIFF, a hierarchical contextual reasoning framework for multimodal time series forecasting under noisy and weakly aligned documents. SCENARIODIFF organizes contextual information into three levels: a Historical Context Agent extracts stepwise evidence from raw documents, a Scenario Agent produces a qualitative scenario description for the forecast horizon, and an Anchor Guidance Agent generates sparse anchor points for event-relevant future regions. These structured signals condition a Multimodal Diffusion Transformer, while Anchor Blended Sampling locally refines generated trajectories without retraining. Experiments on the Time-MMD benchmark show that SCENARIODIFF is especially effective in event-driven domains, demonstrating the value of explicit hierarchical scenario guidance for multimodal time series forecasting. Our full implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ScenarioDiff_ICDM-2C4C

</details>


### [74] [Population Health-Based Machine Learning Reveals Associations Between Psychosocial Factors and Chronic Kidney Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17174)
*Md. Atik Shams,David Eisenberg,Sumaiya Fatema,Asma Sultana,D. M Hasibul Islam,Junnatul Mawa,Anindita Datta,Nafiya Ahmed,Danastan Tasaouf Mridula,SK. Sazid Mahmud,Simon Bin Akter,Tanjila Helaly,Jorge Fresneda Fernandez,Humayera Islam,Tanmoy Sarkar Pias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用大规模电话健康调查数据(BRFSS和NHIS)结合机器学习,构建了CKD自我报告状态分类模型,并通过SHAP分析识别关键预测因子,为CKD风险分层提供可解释框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CKD隐匿进展严重影响生活质量,早期检测对改善预后至关重要,但现有筛查方法有限,需利用大规模人口调查数据开发高效、可解释的风险预测工具。

Method: 使用BRFSS(2019/2021)和NHIS(2020/2021)四个数据集,共约91万样本;采用九种先进插补方法处理缺失值,采样策略缓解类别不平衡;构建定制堆叠集成模型进行分类;利用SHAP值分析特征重要性,经临床专家复核确定关键预测因子。

Result: 堆叠集成模型在四个数据集上平衡准确率达72.56%-76.12%,AUROC为79.59%-82.29%;SHAP分析显示定期体检、年龄、血压、心理健康压力指标为关键预测因子。

Conclusion: 研究建立了稳健可解释的CKD风险分层框架,证实了常规体检、年龄、血压及心理健康等因素的重要性,为早期筛查和干预提供可操作见解。

Abstract: Chronic kidney disease (CKD) progresses silently and severely undermines quality of life, making early detection critical for improving patient outcomes. We present a two-part study that combines large-scale telehealth data with advanced machine learning to both classify self-reported CKD status and identify key drivers of disease. Using selected features from the Behavioral Risk Factor Surveillance System (BRFSS 2021: 438,693 samples; BRFSS 2019: 418,268 samples) and the National Health Interview Survey (NHIS 2021: 29,482 samples; NHIS 2020: 31,568 samples), we addressed missing data with nine state-of-the-art imputation methods and mitigated class imbalance via sampling strategies. Our customized stacked ensemble model achieved balanced accuracy of 72.56-76.12%, with corresponding AUROC scores of 79.59-82.29%. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) analysis, followed by clinical review, highlighted critical predictors, including regular medical check-ups, age, blood pressure, and indicators of mental health stress. These findings deliver a robust and interpretable framework for CKD risk stratification and provide actionable insights into its associated factors.

</details>


### [75] [Task Specialization Fine-Tuning for Contextual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17180)
*Jianan Zhou,Jung-Hoon Cho,Tianyue Zhou,Han Zheng,Jie Zhang,Roy Dong,Yining Ma,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Task Specialization Fine-Tuning (TSFT) 框架，解决预训练后的上下文强化学习中细粒度预算分配问题，通过参数化性能预测模型和整数线性规划实现样本高效的多任务专业化微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文强化学习（CRL）多从头训练，要么学习单一多任务策略，要么策略性训练多策略。预训练+微调新范式虽符合现代趋势，但面临异质边际收益和样本低效挑战：给定预训练策略和有限预算，如何为每个任务区域分配微调资源以最大化任务覆盖率，是关键开放问题。

Method: TSFT 是一个在线框架：(1) 用简单参数化模型（如对数或幂律）拟合各任务区域的微调性能曲线，预测边际收益；(2) 将离散预算分配建模为整数线性规划（ILP）问题并精确求解；(3) 交替执行数据收集、模型更新和预算重优化，实现自适应资源分配。

Result: 在组合优化、连续控制和大语言模型微调三大决策领域的大量实验中，TSFT 在任务覆盖率指标上显著优于基线方法，且性能接近知晓真实收益曲线的神谕基线，验证了参数化预测与 ILP 分配的有效性。

Conclusion: TSFT 为基于模型的上下文强化学习开辟了新方向，证明了在预训练-微调范式下，通过性能预测建模与最优预算分配相结合，可实现样本高效的多任务专业化，契合当前大模型时代的训练流程。

Abstract: Contextual Reinforcement Learning (CRL) seeks to generalize classical RL by maximizing task coverage across a context space of related tasks. While prior works often train from scratch and rely on either multi-task learning for a single policy or strategically training multiple policies, we advocate for a unified alternative: pretraining a single policy with good initial performance, followed by fine-tuning multiple policies for task specialization. This new paradigm, however, introduces unique challenges, such as heterogeneous marginal returns and sample inefficiency. This raises a critical research question: given a pretrained policy and a constrained budget, how much fine-tuning should each task region receive to enable sample-efficient CRL? To this end, we propose Task Specialization Fine-Tuning (TSFT), an online framework that predicts fine-tuning performance with a simple parametric model and exactly solves the resulting discrete budget allocation problem via integer linear programming. Extensive experiments across diverse decision domains, including combinatorial optimization, continuous control, and LLM fine-tuning, demonstrate that TSFT significantly outperforms baselines in task coverage and approaches oracle performance. Our work charts a new direction for model-based CRL, aligning with the modern pretrain-finetune era.

</details>


### [76] [Reinforcement Learning as (Discrete) Potential Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17181)
*Christopher Connolly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了强化学习与势理论的深层联系，在固定策略假设下建立了基于势理论的RL表示和算法框架，并指出该框架可自然延伸至非线性情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习理论根本依赖于马尔可夫链的概率论，而概率论与势理论存在深层联系。作者旨在利用势理论视角重新审视核心RL表示和算法，寻求提高样本效率和形式化约束的新路径。

Method: 在固定策略假设下，回顾概率论与势理论的联系，构建线性势理论框架分析RL的核心表示和算法；随后将框架自然延伸至非线性情形以放松固定策略假设。

Result: 建立了基于势理论的RL理论框架，该视角可为强化学习提供改进的样本效率和形式化约束；线性势理论框架在放松固定策略假设后可自然延伸至非线性情况。

Conclusion: 势理论为强化学习提供了新的理论视角和数学工具，有望在样本效率和算法约束方面带来改进，且框架具有从线性到非线性的良好扩展性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) theory fundamentally depends on probability theory through the Markov chain. There is a deep connection between probability theory and potential theory. This paper reviews that connection and explores the potential-theoretic viewpoint for core reinforcement learning representations and algorithms under a fixed-policy assumption. This viewpoint may offer a path for improved sample efficiency and formal constraints that can be applied to RL. When the fixed-policy assumption is relaxed, the linear potential theory framework can be naturally extended to the nonlinear case.

</details>


### [77] [How smoothing the affinity matrix affects neighborhood preservation in t-SNE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17190)
*Shirin Mohebi,Guillaume Bied,Jefrey Lijffijt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了t-SNE亲和矩阵中概率分布的尖锐度如何影响不同尺度下的邻域保持，提出了一种由参数gamma控制的逐行幂变换，该变换在保持稀疏性和秩序的同时平滑或锐化亲和矩阵的每一行。实验表明，锐化提高了最近邻的保持，而平滑提高了更宽局部邻域的保持，在中局部范围内优于包括多尺度方法在内的替代亲和构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-SNE作为可视化高维数据最广泛使用的方法之一，其核心组件是亲和矩阵，现有方法缺乏对亲和矩阵中概率分布尖锐度如何影响不同尺度邻域保持的系统研究，且现有的多尺度方法在中局部范围表现不足。

Method: 引入逐行幂变换，由参数gamma控制，可平滑或锐化亲和矩阵每行的概率分布，同时保持稀疏性和秩序。证明该变换等价于重新缩放高斯带宽即改变困惑度，但由于每个点的分布尖锐度不同，固定gamma会导致点依赖的有效困惑度，这与全局改变困惑度不同。

Result: 锐化（gamma > 1）显著改善最近邻的保持；平滑（gamma < 1）改善更宽局部邻域的保持；在中局部范围内，该方法优于包括多尺度方法在内的其他亲和矩阵构造方法。

Conclusion: 逐行幂变换为t-SNE提供了一种灵活控制不同尺度邻域保持的方法：锐化适合极局部结构，平滑适合较宽局部结构，在中局部范围内超越现有多尺度方法，为t-SNE亲和矩阵设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction methods are instrumental to visualize high-dimensional data, and t-SNE stands as one of the most widely used methods due to its emphasis on local neighborhood preservation. A central component of t-SNE is the affinity matrix, which expresses pairwise similarities in the form of symmetrized probabilities, over which the optimization problem of t-SNE is defined. We study how the sharpness of this probability distribution affects neighborhood preservation at different scales. We introduce a row-wise power transform controlled by a parameter gamma that can smooth or sharpen each row of the affinity matrix while preserving sparsity and rank order. We show that this transform is equivalent to rescaling the Gaussian bandwidth and thus to changing the perplexity. However, as the sharpness of the probability distribution varies per point, a fixed gamma leads to point-dependent effective perplexities, making it distinct from changing the global perplexity. Empirically, we find that sharpening improves preservation of the very nearest neighbors, while smoothing improves preservation of broader local neighborhoods, outperforming alternative affinity constructions including multiscale methods in the mid-local range.

</details>


### [78] [Pessimistic Meta-Induction and Its Limits: Lessons from Frequentist Statistics and Machine Learning Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17213)
*Hanti Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入频率学派统计、机器学习和形式认识论中的收敛性概念，反驳了悲观元归纳论证：普通枚举归纳可实现“处处收敛”，而元归纳甚至无法实现“几乎处处收敛”，且在该问题情境下没有任何推断方法能达成几乎处处收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对科学实在论面临的悲观元归纳论证，现有反驳多集中于历史前提，作者旨在从归纳推理的认知规范性出发，直接削弱其归纳步骤的合理性。

Method: 借用频率学派统计、机器学习和形式认识论中“收敛于真理”的框架，将归纳推理的评价标准设定为收敛性，比较普通枚举归纳与元归纳在收敛性能上的差异。

Result: 普通枚举归纳能够达到处处收敛；元归纳甚至无法达到几乎处处收敛；更深层地，在元归纳所在的问题情境中，不存在任何能实现几乎处处收敛的推断方法。

Conclusion: 悲观元归纳论证的归纳步骤在认识论上站不住脚，因为它所依赖的元层面归纳推理比普通归纳更不可靠，甚至在原则上无法被任何方法挽救，从而为科学实在论提供了新的防御路径。

Abstract: This paper challenges the pessimistic meta-inductive argument against scientific realism by undermining its inductive step rather than its historical premise. Although related challenges already exist, I develop a new one. Drawing on a general epistemology of scientific inference developed in frequentist statistics, machine learning, and formal epistemology, I evaluate induction in terms of convergence to the truth. I argue that ordinary enumerative induction can achieve everywhere convergence, whereas meta-induction fails even to achieve almost everywhere convergence. Indeed, in the problem context where meta-induction arises, the failure is deeper: no inference method whatsoever achieves almost everywhere convergence.

</details>


### [79] [Delta2Gamma: Band-Wise Adaptive Contrastive Learning of EEG for Alzheimer's Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17231)
*Chanwoo Park,Chanwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Delta2Gamma，一种用于EEG的自监督学习框架，通过将脑电信号分解为五大经典神经节律（delta、theta、alpha、beta、gamma）并为每个频带配备独立编码器和投影头，结合自适应温度机制平衡不同频带的信号统计特性，在ADFTD队列上以留一被试出协议实现阿尔茨海默症与认知正常对照的92.4%分类准确率，超越监督基线和现有EEG专用方法，为低成本、可规模化的痴呆筛查提供新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低成本、可大规模部署的痴呆筛查难题。影像学诊断昂贵且难以普及，EEG虽便携廉价但信号嘈杂、被试间差异大且临床标签稀缺，现有方法难以有效利用无标签EEG数据进行表征学习。

Method: Delta2Gamma自监督框架：1) 将每段EEG记录分解为五大经典神经节律频带；2) 每个频带配备独立编码器和投影头；3) 引入自适应温度参数，在对比训练中动态预测，自动平衡不同频带的信号统计特性；4) 通过对比增强视图学习无标签EEG的表征。

Result: 在ADFTD队列上采用严格的留一被试出协议，Delta2Gamma将阿尔茨海默症与认知正常对照分离准确率达92.4%，显著优于监督学习主干网络及近期专用EEG方法。

Conclusion: Delta2Gamma通过频带分解与自适应对比学习有效克服了EEG信号噪声大、被试差异大、标签稀缺等挑战，实现了最先进的痴呆分类性能，证明了基于EEG的低成本、可规模化痴呆筛查的临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Low-cost, scalable screening for dementia remains an open problem. Imaging-based diagnosis is costly and hard to deploy widely. Electroencephalography (EEG) is portable and inexpensive, but its recordings are noisy, vary widely across subjects, and carry few clinical labels. We tackle this with Delta2Gamma, a self-supervised framework that learns EEG representations from unlabeled data by contrasting augmented views of each signal. Rather than treat EEG as a single stream, Delta2Gamma decomposes every recording into the five canonical neural rhythms (delta, theta, alpha, beta, gamma). Each band gets its own encoder and projection head. Each also gets a temperature that is predicted adaptively during contrastive training, so bands with different signal statistics are balanced automatically. On the ADFTD cohort under a strict leave-one-subject-out protocol, Delta2Gamma separates Alzheimer's disease from cognitively normal controls with 92.4\% accuracy. This exceeds both supervised backbones and recent dedicated EEG methods.

</details>


### [80] [Physics-Informed and Hybrid Machine Learning in Additive Manufacturing: Application to Fused Filament Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17246)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Sankaran Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了三种将物理知识融入深度神经网络的策略，用于预测熔丝制造(FFF)零件的结合质量和孔隙率，通过八种组合实验表明多策略结合可在有限实验数据下构建准确模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决纯数据驱动深度学习模型在FFF工艺中缺乏物理一致性、实验数据稀缺导致预测不可靠的问题，需将多物理场仿真结果与物理约束融入模型以保证预测符合物理规律。

Method: 提出三类物理融合策略：(1) 在损失函数中加入物理约束；(2) 将物理模型输出作为DNN额外输入；(3) 用物理模型输入输出预训练DNN再用实验数据微调。共测试八种策略组合。

Result: 实验表明，多策略组合能显著提升模型在有限实验数据下的预测精度，强制建立结合质量与拉伸强度的物理一致关系，使孔隙率预测具有物理意义。

Conclusion: 物理信息融合的混合机器学习方法能有效解决FFF工艺中数据稀缺与物理一致性矛盾，多策略协同优于单一策略，为增材制造质量预测提供了可推广的框架。

Abstract: This article investigates several physics-informed and hybrid machine learning strategies that incorporate physics knowledge in experimental data-driven deep-learning models for predicting the bond quality and porosity of fused filament fabrication (FFF) parts. Three types of strategies are explored to incorporate physics constraints and multi-physics FFF simulation results into a deep neural network (DNN), thus ensuring consistency with physical laws: (1) incorporate physics constraints within the loss function of the DNN, (2) use physics model outputs as additional inputs to the DNN model, and (3) pre-train a DNN model with physics model input-output and then update it with experimental data. These strategies help to enforce a physically consistent relationship between bond quality and tensile strength, thus making porosity predictions physically meaningful. Eight different combinations of the above strategies are investigated. The results show how the combination of multiple strategies produces accurate machine learning models even with limited experimental data.

</details>


### [81] [Co-RL: Unsupervised Reasoning Emerges from Diverse Cohort in Multi-agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17253)
*Yunhao Yang,Yuexin Bian,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Tianjin Huang,Yuanyuan Shi,Ziang Xiao,Nuno Vasconcelos,Yijiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Co-RL 是一种协作多智能体强化学习框架，通过多个解耦模型互相提供奖励信号，在无监督条件下实现推理能力提升，超越基础模型和无标签方法，媲美甚至超过有监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习依赖昂贵的人工标注奖励，自我奖励 RL 会强化偏见、降低多样性并导致训练崩溃，需要一种无需真实标签且能保持多样性的方法。

Method: 提出 Co-RL 框架，多个无共享参数的模型同时通过 RL 优化，奖励来自同伴模型；通过异构模型家族、规模和重述训练样本增加群体多样性，减少相关误差。

Result: 在七个纯文本基准上平均提升 3.0-8.6%，四个多模态基准上提升 2.3-7.2%，持续优于基础模型和无标签方法，匹配或超越有监督方法，且无需任何真实标签。

Conclusion: 协作多智能体训练结合群体多样性可有效缓解自我强化偏见，实现无监督推理涌现，为无标签强化学习提供新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful approach for improving reasoning in language and vision-language models, yet its strongest successes still depend heavily on ground-truth supervision (e.g., verifiable reward). Such annotations are costly to obtain and become increasingly scarce as reasoning capabilities advance beyond what humans can reliably evaluate. Self-rewarding RL reduces this dependence by enabling models to derive reward signals from their own completions. However, training solely on self-generated feedback can reinforce existing biases and suboptimal behaviors, reduce response diversity, and ultimately lead to homogenized responses and training collapse. In this work, we show that unsupervised reasoning can emerge through cooperative multi-agent training. We introduce Co-RL, a framework in which multiple decoupled models, sharing no parameters, are simultaneously optimized through RL using rewards derived from their peers. We further show that increasing cohort diversity, through heterogeneous model families, sizes, and rephrased training samples, reduces the correlated errors that drive self-reinforcing feedback loops. This diversity consistently improves reasoning performance, maintains behavioral diversity, and mitigates training collapse. Across text-only and multimodal domains, Co-RL consistently outperforms the base models and prior label-free approaches, while matching or surpassing supervised methods, without access to any ground-truth labels. Concretely, Co-RL yields average gains of 3.0-8.6% across seven text-only benchmarks for LLMs and 2.3-7.2% across four multimodal benchmarks for VLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/DrStranded/Co-RL.

</details>


### [82] [Understanding Curriculum Learning in Large Language Models via Cross-Difficulty Optimization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17268)
*Zhikai Ding,Ziyi Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了课程学习在大语言模型后训练中的优化动力学，提出了跨难度知识迁移的量化指标——相对迁移，并据此设计了迁移感知动态课程采样（TDCS），在多个推理基准上持续优于现有调度策略，为课程学习提供了统一的优化视角解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程学习在大模型后训练中被广泛采用，但其有效性在不同推理任务间差异巨大，不存在通用的最优课程，因此需要从优化动力学角度理解何时课程学习有效，并设计自适应的课程调度方法。

Method: 通过分析不同课程调度引发的优化动力学，形式化定义了跨难度知识迁移的度量——相对迁移；基于此提出迁移感知动态课程采样（TDCS），在训练过程中动态估计迁移关系并调整采样分布。

Result: 在多个推理基准、不同模型规模和训练范式下的大量实验表明，TDCS 一致优于代表性的调度策略，验证了基于跨难度迁移的优化解释的有效性。

Conclusion: 本工作为课程学习提供了基于跨难度迁移的统一优化解释框架，并提出了自适应的 TDCS 方法，显著提升了课程学习在推理任务上的鲁棒性和泛化性。

Abstract: Curriculum learning has been widely adopted in the post-training of large language models by organizing training data from easy to hard. However, its effectiveness varies substantially across reasoning tasks, suggesting that no single curriculum is universally optimal and raising a fundamental question: what determines when curriculum learning works? In this paper, we answer this question by analyzing the optimization dynamics induced by different curriculum schedules. We show that the transfer relationship between different difficulty levels characterizes the optimization dynamics induced by curriculum learning, which in turn explains the effectiveness of different curriculum schedules, and formalize this relationship as Relative Transfer, a principled measure of cross-difficulty knowledge transfer. Based on this measurement, we derive Transfer-aware Dynamic Curriculum Sampling (TDCS), which dynamically adjusts the sampling distribution according to the estimated transfer relationship throughout training. Extensive experiments on multiple reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that TDCS consistently outperforms representative scheduling strategies across different tasks, model scales, and training paradigms. More importantly, our work provides a unified optimization-based explanation of curriculum learning through cross-difficulty transfer.

</details>


### [83] [Rethinking Irregular Time Series Forecasting from the Perspective of Basis Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17284)
*Rongwen Li,Changjian Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种去偏神经基函数网络（DNBNet），通过重要性采样校正渐近偏差，并用神经网络参数化基函数以适应多样时间模式，结合多尺度分解和双分支解码器，在不规则时间序列预测任务上取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将不规则观测聚合为固定维度的估计响应系数，但存在两个关键局限：忽略时间戳采样密度导致的渐近偏差，以及预定义基函数对多样时间模式的适应性有限。

Method: 核心是去偏神经基函数响应机制，利用重要性采样校正渐近偏差，并用神经网络参数化基函数；设计基于平均池化的多尺度分解模块和质量感知融合机制获取更丰富表示；采用双分支解码器进行预测。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验证明了DNBNet的有效性及其在多样不规则时间序列场景中的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: DNBNet通过解决采样密度导致的偏差和基函数适应性问题，显著提升了不规则时间序列预测性能，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Irregular time series forecasting is crucial in many domains, such as healthcare and meteorological observation. However, due to the inherent characteristics of irregular time series, including sparse observations and non-uniform sampling, accurately predicting future dynamics remains challenging. In light of these two characteristics, many existing methods aggregate irregular observations into fixed-dimensional estimated response coefficients through predefined basis functions and use these coefficients as sequence representations. Nevertheless, this modeling paradigm still suffers from two key limitations: (i) a potential non-vanishing asymptotic bias caused by ignoring the sampling density of timestamps; and (ii) the limited adaptability of predefined basis functions to diverse temporal patterns. In this study, we propose a Debiased Neural Basis-Function Network (DNBNet) to address these challenges. Its core is a debiased neural basis-function response mechanism, which corrects asymptotic bias through importance sampling while parameterizing basis functions with neural networks to adapt to diverse temporal patterns. In addition, considering the sparsity of irregular data, we design a novel multi-scale decomposition module based on average pooling, together with a mass-aware fusion mechanism, to obtain richer representations. Finally, a dual-branch decoder is employed for forecasting. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of DNBNet and its strong generalizability across diverse irregular time series scenarios. Our code can be obtained at https://github.com/hnu-vis/DNBNet.

</details>


### [84] [Abra: Scaling Diffusion Image Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17286)
*Kyle Chickering,Wei-An Lin,Swayam Bhanded,Dan Saunders,Akshat Tripathi,Jiaming Song,Shyamal Buch,Xinchen Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了文生图扩散模型的计算最优缩放定律，使用Abra这一流匹配Transformer家族，在10^19到10^22 FLOPs的三个数量级算力范围内进行训练。发现扩散模型像语言模型一样具有可预测的缩放行为，但计算最优需要约200个图像token/参数，是Chinchilla定律的10倍；扩散模型对过度训练具有鲁棒性，建议优先增加数据而非模型规模；这种可预测性延伸到生成质量指标、最优CFG设置、表征质量甚至训练曲线形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然计算最优缩放定律已指导前沿语言模型训练，但视觉生成领域的缩放定律仍 largely unexplored（很大程度上未被探索）。需要系统研究扩散模型的缩放行为，以指导大规模文生图模型的最优资源分配。

Method: 构建Abra——一个受控的流匹配Transformer模型家族，在三个数量级的算力预算（10^19到10^22 FLOPs）上系统训练，覆盖显著大于以往工作的计算规模。通过大量实验测量训练损失、生成质量指标、最优CFG设置、表征质量及训练曲线形状随模型规模和数据量的变化规律。

Result: 1) 扩散模型缩放像语言模型一样可预测；2) 计算最优点约为200图像token/参数，比LLM的Chinchilla定律大10倍；3) 扩散模型对过度训练鲁棒，实践者应倾向于更多数据而非更大模型；4) 可预测性延伸至生成质量、最优CFG、表征质量及训练曲线普适形状。

Conclusion: 文生图扩散模型遵循清晰的计算最优缩放定律，但与语言模型在数据需求上存在根本差异。这为大规模视觉生成模型的高效训练提供了理论指导：在给定算力下，应分配更多资源到数据而非模型参数，且无需担心过度训练带来的性能下降。

Abstract: Compute-optimal scaling laws guide the training of frontier language models yet remain largely unexplored for visual generation. We present a systematic scaling law study for text-to-image diffusion models using Abra, a controlled family of flow-matching transformers trained across three orders of magnitude worth of compute ($10^{19}$ to $10^{22}$ FLOPs), reaching significantly larger compute budgets than previous works. We demonstrate that diffusion models scale just as predictably as language models but require far more data to train optimally: compute optimality occurs at approximately $200$ image tokens per parameter, ten times the Chinchilla compute-optimal prescription for LLMs. We show that unlike language models, diffusion models are robust to overtraining and that practitioners should err on the side of more data rather than a larger model. Finally, we show that this predictability extends beyond training loss to generative quality metrics, optimal CFG settings, representation quality, and even the shape of the training curves, which collapse onto a universal form.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond MSE: Rethinking the Evaluation Metric and Benchmarking for Irregular Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17293)
*Rongwen Li,Haixin Xie,Xiao Wang,Changjian Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出不规则时间序列预测中MSE受时间戳采样分布影响导致评估偏差，提出基于重要性加权的连续时间平方误差（CSE）消除该影响，理论证明CSE渐近估计误差优于MSE，并构建系统基准验证CSE有效性，实验表明CSE能更准确恢复连续时间风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不规则时间序列预测研究主要关注模型设计，评估指标研究不足。基准通常用MSE，但MSE在不规则预测中不仅取决于模型预测，还受样本特定时间戳采样分布影响，导致对模型连续时间预测性能的评估存在偏差。

Method: 提出连续时间平方误差（CSE），利用重要性加权消除时间戳采样分布的影响；理论证明CSE关于连续时间风险的渐近估计误差不大于MSE；构建包含合成、半合成及八个真实数据集的系统基准，验证CSE有效性并系统评估模型连续时间预测性能。

Result: 实验表明CSE能比MSE更准确地恢复连续时间风险；仅依赖MSE可能无法完全反映模型在真实场景下的连续时间预测性能。

Conclusion: CSE为不规则时间序列预测提供了更公平、更准确的评估指标，解决了MSE受时间戳采样分布偏差的问题，有助于更真实地评估模型连续时间预测能力。

Abstract: Existing research on irregular time-series forecasting has primarily focused on model design, while evaluation metrics remain insufficiently studied. Existing benchmarks typically use mean squared error (MSE) as the evaluation metric. We show that, in irregular forecasting, MSE is determined not only by the model prediction but also by the sample-specific timestamp sampling distributions, leading to a biased assessment of the models' continuous-time predictive performance. To address this issue, we propose the Continuous-time Squared Error (CSE), which employs importance weighting to eliminate the influence of the timestamp sampling distributions. We further theoretically prove that CSE's asymptotic estimation error with respect to continuous-time risk is no greater than that of MSE. Finally, we construct a systematic benchmark covering synthetic, semi-synthetic, and eight real-world datasets to validate the effectiveness of CSE and systematically evaluate models' continuous-time predictive performance. Experiments show that CSE can recover continuous-time risk more accurately than MSE, while relying solely on MSE may not fully reflect models' continuous-time predictive performance in real-world scenarios. Our code can be obtained at https://github.com/hnu-vis/ITS-Bench.

</details>


### [86] [Agentic ESOpt: Fine-Tuning Long-Horizon LLM Agents with Minimal GPU Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17310)
*Zhi Zheng,Rongsheng Chen,Yunpeng Ba,Zhenkun Wang,Yee Whye Teh,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic ESOpt框架，利用进化策略（ES）进行长时程LLM智能体的全参数微调，相比强化学习（RL）在模型可扩展性、灵活性和长时程可扩展性上具有显著优势，在WebArena-Lite上Qwen-3.5-27B性能提升6.69%，在36个测试设置中28个优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在单轮LLM微调中表现良好，但在长时程智能体推理中面临分支交互增加和稀疏奖励的挑战：基于反向传播的训练栈过于庞大，难以微调大型LLM；长时程轨迹使得RL中的信用分配变得更加困难。

Method: Agentic ESOpt是一个全参数智能体微调框架，支持参数-上下文协同进化。核心步骤：1）在当前LLM参数周围采样扰动；2）用奖励评估生成的智能体；3）应用在线奖励加权更新。引入余弦衰减调度的扰动尺度σ来改善探索-适应权衡。

Result: 在WebArena-Lite基准上，Qwen-3.5-27B的全参数优化使无技能基线提升6.69%。在测试时自动启发式设计中，Agentic ESOpt执行在线提示-参数协同进化，在36个设置中有28个优于匹配基线。

Conclusion: 进化策略（ES）是长时程LLM智能体微调的更好选择，提供三大关键优势：模型可扩展性（仅需推理级GPU内存即可全参数优化）、灵活性（轻量级黑盒反馈接口易于与提示空间进化组合）、长时程可扩展性（轨迹级参数归因无需跨时程分解奖励，随时程增长扩展性优于Agentic RL）。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has been promising in single-turn LLM fine-tuning. However, long-horizon agentic reasoning introduces increasingly branching interactions and sparse rewards, exposing several limitations of RL: its heavyweight backpropagation-based training stack makes it impractical to fine-tune larger LLMs, and longer-horizon trajectories make credit assignment in RL substantially harder. This paper argues that evolution strategies (ES) can be a better choice for fine-tuning long-horizon LLM agents. Compared with agentic RL, ES offers three key advantages: 1) Model Scalability: ES enables full-parameter optimization with only minimal, inference-level GPU memory, making it possible to fine-tune large LLMs. 2) Flexibility: its lightweight, black-box feedback interface makes ES fine-tuning easy to compose with prompt-space evolution (e.g., skill optimization & test-time compute); and 3) Long-Horizon Scalability: ES performs trajectory-level parameter attribution without decomposing rewards across horizons, yielding better scalability than Agentic RL as the horizon length grows. Based on this insight, we propose Agentic ESOpt, a full-parameter agentic fine-tuning framework tailored to flexible parameter--context co-evolution. At each step, Agentic ESOpt samples perturbations around the current LLM parameters, evaluates the resulting agents with rewards, and applies an online reward-weighted update. To improve the exploration--adaptation trade-off, Agentic ESOpt further introduces a cosine decay schedule of the perturbation scale $σ$. On WebArena-Lite, full-parameter optimization of Qwen-3.5-27B improves the No Skill baseline by 6.69%. In test-time automatic heuristic design, Agentic ESOpt performs online prompt--parameter co-evolution, improving its matched baseline in 28 of 36 settings.

</details>


### [87] [MoFE: A Novel Mixture-of-Experts Framework with Fourier Neural Operators for Cryptocurrency Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17342)
*Bowen Liu,Mingming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MoFE框架，融合傅里叶神经算子（FNO）与混合专家（MoE）架构，针对加密货币价格预测中的非平稳性、相位滞后等难题，在比特币数据上实现T+1/T+5预测SOTA，并通过高保真模拟交易验证了显著超额收益与高夏普比率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币价格预测面临内在非平稳性、突发制度转换、多尺度随机依赖等核心挑战，传统深度学习模型难以捕捉复杂底层动态，常导致持续的相位滞后预测，亟需一种能自适应建模多频率分量并跨市场制度切换的新方法。

Method: 基于随机微分方程理论，将波动率建模为用户网络基本面增长、挖矿成本与减半机制导致的季节性波动、市场情绪诱导的混沌三者的多频率叠加。设计自适应傅里叶神经算子（AFNO）与卷积双域专家学习连续函数到函数的映射，分别捕捉全局频谱趋势、周期性调整与微观结构；引入动态门控MoE机制，实现跨多样化市场制度的自适应策略切换。

Result: 在2020-2025年比特币数据集上，MoFE在T+1与T+5两个预测视野均达到SOTA，有效缓解相位滞后效应，方向准确率（DA）与信息系数（IC）显著优于基线。高保真模拟交易环境中，预测优势转化为统计显著的超额收益与稳健的风险调整后表现，夏普比率显著提升。

Conclusion: MoFE通过将频谱分析与混合专家架构深度融合，成功捕捉加密货币市场的多尺度随机动态，实现了高精度价格预测与可落地的量化交易策略，为金融时间序列的非平稳建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Forecasting cryptocurrency prices remains a formidable challenge due to inherent non-stationarity, abrupt regime shifts, and multi-scale stochastic dependencies. Conventional deep learning models often struggle to capture complex underlying dynamics, frequently resulting in persistent phase-lagged predictions. To address these limitations, we propose MoFE, a novel deep learning framework that integrates Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) within a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture. Rooted in the theoretical framework of stochastic differential equations, MoFE conceptualizes cryptocurrency volatility as a superposition of multi-frequency components, which includes user network based fundamental growth, mining costs and halving mechanism caused seasonal volatility, and market sentiment-induced chaos. Specifically, specialized adaptive FNO (AFNO) and Convolution dual-domain experts learn continuous function-to-function mappings to encapsulate global spectral trends, cyclical adjustments and microstructures, while a dynamic gating based MoE mechanism enables adaptive strategy switching across diverse market regimes. Extensive experiments on Bitcoin datasets spanning January 2020 to December 2025 demonstrate that MoFE achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in both T+1 and T+5 forecasting horizons. Notably, the model effectively mitigates the phase-lag effect, delivering superior Directional Accuracy (DA) and Information Coefficient (IC). In high-fidelity simulated trading environments, these predictive gains transfer into significant excess returns and robust risk-adjusted performance, characterized by a high Sharpe ratio.

</details>


### [88] [Tight Bounds for Data-driven Multiple Hyper-parameter Tuning with Structured Loss Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17343)
*Anh Tuan Nguyen,Viet Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文利用实代数几何和拓扑方法，建立了多维数据驱动超参数调优的紧伪维度界，解决了现有泛化保证松散且缺乏下界的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动算法设计将超参数调优建模为统计学习问题，但模型性能对超参数的隐式非光滑依赖使得泛化保证难以建立；现有的分段多项式假设下的多维边界理论上松散，且缺乏全面的下界分析。

Method: 1. 利用实代数几何分析块消除过程中的不变连通符号单元，而非孤立符号向量，避免拓扑过计数，得到更锐利的样本复杂度上界。2. 提出多机制下界框架，分离组合容量与代数容量，通过构建跨不同机制的可粉碎问题实例，证明上界紧致饱和。3. 将拓扑框架扩展至一般的双层验证损失调优及更广泛的半代数应用。

Result: 建立了多维数据驱动调优的紧伪维度界：上界比现有结果更锐利，下界证明上界在多个机制下均达到饱和，且框架成功推广至双层验证损失调优和一般半代数设定。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上解决了数据驱动超参数调优的泛化保证问题，提供了紧致的上下界匹配结果，并通过拓扑-代数方法统一了多种调优场景的分析框架。

Abstract: Data-driven algorithm design frames hyperparameter tuning as a statistical learning problem, but establishing generalization guarantees remains challenging due to the implicit, non-smooth dependence of model performance on hyperparameters. Existing multi-dimensional bounds under piecewise-polynomial assumptions remain theoretically loose and lack comprehensive lower bounds. We resolve this by establishing tight pseudo-dimension bounds for multi-dimensional data-driven tuning. First, we refine the learning-theoretic upper bound using real algebraic geometry; by analyzing invariant connected sign cells during block elimination rather than isolated sign vectors, we avoid topological over-counting to derive strictly sharper sample complexities. Second, we present a multi-regime lower-bound framework that disentangles combinatorial and algebraic capacities. By constructing shattered problem instances across distinct regimes, we prove our upper bounds are tightly saturated. Finally, we extend our topological framework to accommodate general bi-level validation-loss tuning and broader semi-algebraic applications.

</details>


### [89] [Repetition as Reinforcement: Enhancing Sample Efficiency via Instant Episode Repetition in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17347)
*Hoda Yamani,Yuning Xing,Koen van Rijnsoever,Bruce A. MacDonald,Henry Williams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为即时回合重复 (IER) 的新型机制，受人类重复学习启发，通过在环境交互过程中立即重复高奖励回合的动作序列来提高样本效率，并将其集成到 SAC 和 TD3 算法中，在连续控制任务上取得了优于基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类学习中重复机制的启发——重温成功经验能强化记忆、巩固技能并提升未来表现，作者旨在解决传统经验回放和自我模仿学习 (SIL) 等被动复用历史经验的局限，提出一种直接影响数据收集过程的主动机制。

Method: 提出 Instant Episode Repetition (IER)：当检测到高奖励回合时，智能体在后续固定数量的回合中立即重复该回合的动作序列，通过与环境的再次交互来强化有价值的行为。将 IER 集成到最先进的 SAC 和 TD3 算法中，在 MuJoCo、DeepMind Control Suite 以及真实机械臂动态物体搬运任务上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，这一简单机制在连续控制基准测试中显著提升了学习性能，优于标准基线和基于自我模仿学习的基线方法。

Conclusion: IER 作为一种简单且新颖的即时回合重复机制，通过主动重复成功经验直接影响数据收集过程，有效提高了样本效率和学习性能，为强化学习中的探索与利用提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Repetition is a fundamental mechanism in human learning, where revisiting successful experiences strengthens memory, consolidates skills, and improves future performance. Motivated by this biological principle, we introduce Instant Episode Repetition (IER), a simple and novel mechanism that improves sample efficiency by immediately repeating action sequences from successful episodes during environment interaction. Unlike conventional approaches such as Experience Replay and Self-Imitation Learning (SIL), which passively reuse past experience during training updates, IER directly influences the data collection process. Upon identifying a high-reward episode, the agent repeats its action sequence for a fixed number of subsequent episodes, reinforcing valuable behaviors through renewed interaction with the environment. We integrate IER into state-of-the-art SAC and TD3 algorithms and evaluate its effectiveness on continuous-control benchmarks, including MuJoCo, the DeepMind Control Suite, and a real-world dynamic object translation task with a robotic manipulator. Experimental results demonstrate that this simple mechanism improves learning performance over standard and self-imitation-based baselines.

</details>


### [90] [CORAM: Coherent Orthogonal Rotation for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17366)
*Xinyi Sui,Ziran Liu,Nam Ling,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CORAM方法，通过在基础模型SVD框架下对专家切片进行流形平均并引入自适应放大系数，有效合并微调模型，性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法主要在欧几里得权重空间进行线性运算，无法捕捉更新的几何结构；OrthoMerge虽使用正交变换但无法改变奇异值，限制了合并性能。

Method: CORAM将目标矩阵按行分片，在基础模型SVD框架下用奇异值分解表示各专家切片，在对应流形上合并任务特定因子；引入放大系数λ=κĉ补偿流形平均收缩，其中ĉ由专家和合并更新范数估计（约√N），κ从专家更新离散度选择；包含扩散切片分配高更新行和非目标层残差路径。

Result: 在覆盖三个模型家族、3B-9B规模、语言和视觉语言专家的四个测试套件中，CORAM比OrthoMerge提升0.25-1.35分，达到或超越最强权重空间基线。

Conclusion: CORAM通过流形层面的专家因子合并与自适应放大机制，有效解决了模型合并中的几何失真问题，实现了无需联合训练的高性能多任务模型融合。

Abstract: Merging finetuned models combines specialized capabilities without joint training or access to the original data. Most methods operate by linear arithmetic in Euclidean weight space, which cannot carry the geometry of the update. Orthogonal Model Merging (OrthoMerge) uses a single orthogonal transform for each weight matrix, but such a transform cannot change singular values. We propose CORAM, which partitions each target matrix into row slices, represents every expert slice by its singular value decomposition in the corresponding base-model SVD frame, and merges the task-specific factors on their corresponding manifolds. Because manifold averaging contracts the merged update, CORAM applies an amplification coefficient $λ=κ\hat{c}$. The scale c_hat is estimated from the expert and merged update norms and is approximately $\sqrt{N}$ for $N$ experts with comparable update magnitudes. The restoration strength kappa is selected from the dispersion of expert updates without evaluating candidate merged models. This rule remains within 0.72 points of the best swept value on all evaluated suites. CORAM also includes spread slicing to distribute highly updated rows across slices and a residual pathway for non-target layers. Across four suites covering three model families, 3B to 9B scales, and language and vision-language experts, CORAM improves over OrthoMerge by 0.25 to 1.35 points and matches or exceeds the strongest weight-space baselines.

</details>


### [91] [Pathology Transport: Optimal-Transport Explanations for Clinical Data, and When Their Heatmaps (Fail to) Localize Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17370)
*Lalit Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于最优传输修正流的生成式解释方法，在表格肿瘤生物标志物和胸部X光片上测试，发现合成病变上的解释热图看似有效，但在真实放射科医生标注框上失效，揭示了合成到真实的泛化鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过建模健康与患病患者分布的几何关系，提供可解释的临床AI，而非仅探测分类器；并严格测试生成的解释热图是否真能定位疾病。

Method: 构建最优传输修正流连接两个临床分布，用于表格数据（乳腺癌威斯康星数据集）和胸部X光片；引入基于重建的身份保持变体以定位合成病变；使用点击游戏、AUROC、与监督分类器归因相关性等指标评估。

Result: 表格数据上：单一流产生患者级反事实、无监督恶性评分（AUROC 0.91），无标签归因与监督分类器一致（r ~ 0.5），但预测性能不优于逻辑回归。胸部X光片上：传输热图为群体级信号而非定位器；身份保持变体能定位合成病变（点击游戏0.52），但在真实RSNA放射科医生框上退化到随机水平，仅监督Grad-CAM保持优势。核心发现：合成病变上看似有说服力的无标签热图，不构成真实定位的证据。

Conclusion: 贡献了一个可复用的最优传输生成式解释配方，以及一个受控基准用于压力测试解释方法是否真能定位疾病；强调合成到真实的鸿沟，提醒在合成数据上表现良好的解释不一定泛化到真实临床数据。

Abstract: Generative models promise a route to explainable clinical AI: rather than probe a classifier, model the distributions of healthy and diseased patients and read explanations off the geometry between them. We build such a system - an optimal-transport rectified flow trained between two clinical distributions - and use it to ask a pointed question the field too rarely tests: do the resulting explanation heatmaps actually localize disease? On tabular tumour biomarkers (Breast Cancer Wisconsin) a single flow yields per-patient counterfactuals, an unsupervised malignancy score (AUROC 0.91; 0.93 +/- 0.01 across five seeds), and a label-free attribution that agrees with a supervised classifier (r ~ 0.5) - a compact, honest interpretability engine, though it never out-predicts logistic regression. Moving to chest X-rays, we show the transport heatmap is a population-level signal, not a localiser; a reconstruction-based, identity-preserving variant does localize synthetic lesions (pointing game 0.52), yet on real RSNA radiologist boxes it collapses to chance while only supervised Grad-CAM stays above it. The central result is a synthetic-to-real gap: label-free heatmaps that look compelling on planted lesions are not evidence of real localisation. We contribute a reusable optimal-transport recipe for generative explanations and a controlled benchmark for stress-testing whether they localize.

</details>


### [92] [Integrating Novelty and Surprise for Experience Prioritization and Exploration in Image-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17373)
*Hoda Yamani,Henry Williams,Bruce A. MacDonald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 NSPER 和 NSPER+R，结合新奇性与惊讶度优先经验回放并引入内在奖励，提升基于图像 RL 的样本效率与收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于图像强化学习中的样本效率低问题，传统随机采样导致冗余更新，现有 PER 与内在奖励的结合研究不足，需同时优化经验回放质量与探索效率。

Method: 提出 NSPER，利用新奇性捕捉欠表征状态、惊讶度暴露模型认知缺口，优先回放高价值转移；进一步扩展 NSPER+R，将新奇性与惊讶度作为内在奖励融入策略优化，联合提升回放质量与探索。

Result: 在 DeepMind Control Suite 图像任务上，NSPER 与 NSPER+R 显著优于现有方法，训练效率与收敛速度均得到提升。

Conclusion: 结合新奇性与惊讶度的优先经验回放及内在奖励机制，能有效缓解基于图像 RL 的样本效率瓶颈，为后续研究提供了可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Sample efficiency is a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), particularly in image-based domains where agents must learn from high-dimensional visual inputs. Traditional sampling often relies on random or suboptimal experience selection, leading to redundant updates and slow learning. Improving efficiency requires mechanisms that prioritize informative experiences while also encouraging effective exploration. Prioritized Experience Replay (PER) addresses part of this challenge by reusing high-value transitions, while intrinsic rewards promote the exploration of novel or uncertain states. However, their integration has not been extensively studied. This paper introduces Novelty and Surprise Prioritized Experience Replay (NSPER), which uses novelty to capture underrepresented states and surprise to expose gaps in the agent's understanding of the environment. We further extend this with NSPER+R, integrating these signals as intrinsic rewards to jointly improve replay quality and exploration. Experiments on DeepMind Control Suite tasks show that NSPER and NSPER+R improve training efficiency and convergence speed compared to existing methods in image-based RL.

</details>


### [93] [GUPO: Gradient Uncertainty-aware Policy Optimization for Post-Training Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17411)
*Peizheng Guo,Jianqi Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Yun Fan,Jiahuan Zhou,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Gradient Uncertainty-Aware Policy Optimization (GUPO)，针对 GRPO 中组梯度冲突导致的策略更新低效问题，采用贝叶斯建模将组梯度视为随机变量，利用 Dirichlet 分布估计梯度不确定性，并据此校准各组梯度在聚合中的贡献，实验证明其在多个基准上优于标准 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 在大模型推理后训练中广泛使用，但其直接平均同一 mini-batch 内不同查询产生的组梯度，忽略了组梯度间可能存在的方向冲突。实证分析表明，组梯度冲突往往伴随策略更新效果变差，因此需要一种在冲突情况下仍能给出可靠聚合更新方向的方法。标准 GRPO 将组梯度视为确定性贡献，未考虑其可靠性差异。

Method: 提出 GUPO：1) 在贝叶斯框架下将每个组梯度建模为随机变量并估计其概率分布；2) 基于 Dirichlet 分布推导梯度不确定性；3) 利用不确定性对各组梯度在聚合时的贡献进行加权校准，从而得到更稳健的策略更新方向。

Result: 在多个推理基准上进行大量实验，结果显示 GUPO 能有效缓解组梯度冲突带来的负面影响，显著提升策略更新质量，在多项任务上优于标准 GRPO 及其他基线方法。
